Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在视频质量评估任务上表现良好，但适应新场景成本高昂，需要大规模重新训练和昂贵的平均意见分数标注。作者认为预训练MLLM已提供有用的感知先验，主要挑战在于如何高效地将此先验校准到目标MOS空间。

Method: 提出DPC-VQA框架，采用解耦设计：1）使用冻结的MLLM提供基础质量估计和感知先验；2）引入轻量级校准分支预测残差校正，实现目标场景适应。该设计避免了昂贵的端到端重新训练。

Result: 在用户生成内容和AI生成内容基准测试中，DPC-VQA与代表性基线方法相比具有竞争力，同时仅使用传统MLLM-based VQA方法不到2%的可训练参数，且仅需20%的MOS标签仍保持有效。

Conclusion: DPC-VQA通过解耦感知与校准，有效利用预训练MLLM的感知先验，显著降低了视频质量评估的场景适应成本，为高效VQA系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown promising performance on video quality assessment (VQA) tasks. However, adapting them to new scenarios remains expensive due to large-scale retraining and costly mean opinion score (MOS) annotations. In this paper, we argue that a pretrained MLLM already provides a useful perceptual prior for VQA, and that the main challenge is to efficiently calibrate this prior to the target MOS space. Based on this insight, we propose DPC-VQA, a decoupling perception and calibration framework for video quality assessment. Specifically, DPC-VQA uses a frozen MLLM to provide a base quality estimate and perceptual prior, and employs a lightweight calibration branch to predict a residual correction for target-scenario adaptation. This design avoids costly end-to-end retraining while maintaining reliable performance with lower training and data costs. Extensive experiments on both user-generated content (UGC) and AI-generated content (AIGC) benchmarks show that DPC-VQA achieves competitive performance against representative baselines, while using less than 2% of the trainable parameters of conventional MLLM-based VQA methods and remaining effective with only 20\% of MOS labels. The code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [74] [Detecting and refurbishing ground truth errors during training of deep learning-based echocardiography segmentation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12832)
*Iman Islam,Bram Ruijsink,Andrew J. Reader,Andrew P. King*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了深度学习模型对超声心动图分割中标签错误的鲁棒性，并提出了基于梯度方差(VOG)的错误检测和伪标签修复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分割通常依赖人工标注的ground truth标签，但这些标签可能存在随机错误或系统性偏差。本研究旨在评估深度学习模型对超声心动图分割中标签错误的鲁棒性，并开发有效的错误检测和修复方法。

Method: 使用CAMUS数据集模拟三种错误类型，比较基于损失的错误检测方法和基于梯度方差(VOG)的方法。提出伪标签方法修复疑似错误的GT标签，并在不同错误水平下评估所提方法的性能。

Result: VOG方法在训练期间能有效标记错误的GT标签。标准U-Net在随机标签错误和中等水平系统性错误(最高50%)下保持良好性能。检测和修复方法显著提高了性能，特别是在高错误条件下。

Conclusion: VOG是检测标签错误的有效工具，而检测和修复策略能显著提升模型在标签错误条件下的性能，特别是在高错误率场景下。

Abstract: Deep learning-based medical image segmentation typically relies on ground truth (GT) labels obtained through manual annotation, but these can be prone to random errors or systematic biases. This study examines the robustness of deep learning models to such errors in echocardiography (echo) segmentation and evaluates a novel strategy for detecting and refurbishing erroneous labels during model training. Using the CAMUS dataset, we simulate three error types, then compare a loss-based GT label error detection method with one based on Variance of Gradients (VOG). We also propose a pseudo-labelling approach to refurbish suspected erroneous GT labels. We assess the performance of our proposed approach under varying error levels. Results show that VOG proved highly effective in flagging erroneous GT labels during training. However, a standard U-Net maintained strong performance under random label errors and moderate levels of systematic errors (up to 50%). The detection and refurbishment approach improved performance, particularly under high-error conditions.

</details>


### [75] [Challenging Vision-Language Models with Physically Deployable Multimodal Semantic Lighting Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12833)
*Yingying Zhao,Chengyin Hu,Qike Zhang,Xin Li,Xin Wang,Yiwei Wei,Jiujiang Guo,Jiahuan Long,Tingsong Jiang,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首次提出针对视觉语言模型(VLMs)的物理可部署对抗攻击框架MSLA，通过可控对抗照明破坏多模态语义理解，在真实场景中实现语义攻击而非仅任务特定输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗研究主要关注数字环境，而VLMs在真实世界部署日益增多，物理对抗扰动必须实际可行。物理攻击可能导致识别失败和多模态推理中断，引发下游任务严重语义误解，因此研究VLMs物理攻击对评估其真实世界安全风险至关重要。

Method: 提出多模态语义照明攻击(MSLA)框架，使用可控对抗照明破坏真实场景中的多模态语义理解，攻击语义对齐而非仅任务特定输出。该方法在数字和物理领域进行广泛实验验证。

Result: MSLA能有效降低主流CLIP变体的零样本分类性能，同时在高级VLMs(如LLaVA和BLIP)的图像描述和视觉问答任务中引发严重语义幻觉。实验证明MSLA具有有效性、可迁移性和实际可实现性。

Conclusion: 首次证明VLMs对物理可部署语义攻击高度脆弱，暴露了先前被忽视的鲁棒性差距，强调了对VLMs进行物理世界鲁棒性评估的紧迫需求。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown remarkable performance, yet their security remains insufficiently understood. Existing adversarial studies focus almost exclusively on the digital setting, leaving physical-world threats largely unexplored. As VLMs are increasingly deployed in real environments, this gap becomes critical, since adversarial perturbations must be physically realizable. Despite this practical relevance, physical attacks against VLMs have not been systematically studied. Such attacks may induce recognition failures and further disrupt multimodal reasoning, leading to severe semantic misinterpretation in downstream tasks. Therefore, investigating physical attacks on VLMs is essential for assessing their real-world security risks. To address this gap, we propose Multimodal Semantic Lighting Attacks (MSLA), the first physically deployable adversarial attack framework against VLMs. MSLA uses controllable adversarial lighting to disrupt multimodal semantic understanding in real scenes, attacking semantic alignment rather than only task-specific outputs. Consequently, it degrades zero-shot classification performance of mainstream CLIP variants while inducing severe semantic hallucinations in advanced VLMs such as LLaVA and BLIP across image captioning and visual question answering (VQA). Extensive experiments in both digital and physical domains demonstrate that MSLA is effective, transferable, and practically realizable. Our findings provide the first evidence that VLMs are highly vulnerable to physically deployable semantic attacks, exposing a previously overlooked robustness gap and underscoring the urgent need for physical-world robustness evaluation of VLMs.

</details>


### [76] [PianoFlow: Music-Aware Streaming Piano Motion Generation with Bimanual Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12856)
*Xuan Wang,Kai Ruan,Jiayi Han,kaiyue Zhou,Gaoang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PianoFlow：基于流匹配的钢琴双手运动生成框架，利用MIDI作为训练特权模态，通过角色门控交互模块实现动态跨手协调，支持实时流式生成长序列


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动的钢琴双手运动生成方法存在三个主要问题：1）仅依赖声学表示缺乏符号先验；2）交互机制不灵活；3）只能生成计算成本高的短序列。需要解决这些限制以实现精确协调的双手运动合成

Method: 提出PianoFlow流匹配框架：1）训练时利用MIDI作为特权模态，提取结构化音乐先验实现深度语义理解，推理时仅需音频输入；2）引入非对称角色门控交互模块，通过角色感知注意力和时序门控显式捕获动态跨手协调；3）设计自回归流延续方案，确保跨块时序连贯性，支持任意长度序列的实时流式生成

Result: 在PianoMotion10M数据集上的实验表明，PianoFlow在定量和定性评估上均优于现有方法，同时推理速度比先前方法快9倍以上

Conclusion: PianoFlow通过结合MIDI特权模态学习、角色门控交互机制和自回归流延续方案，成功解决了钢琴双手运动生成的精度、协调性和实时性问题，为音乐驱动的运动合成提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Audio-driven bimanual piano motion generation requires precise modeling of complex musical structures and dynamic cross-hand coordination. However, existing methods often rely on acoustic-only representations lacking symbolic priors, employ inflexible interaction mechanisms, and are limited to computationally expensive short-sequence generation. To address these limitations, we propose PianoFlow, a flow-matching framework for precise and coordinated bimanual piano motion synthesis. Our approach strategically leverages MIDI as a privileged modality during training, distilling these structured musical priors to achieve deep semantic understanding while maintaining audio-only inference. Furthermore, we introduce an asymmetric role-gated interaction module to explicitly capture dynamic cross-hand coordination through role-aware attention and temporal gating. To enable real-time streaming generation for arbitrarily long sequences, we design an autoregressive flow continuation scheme that ensures seamless cross-chunk temporal coherence. Extensive experiments on the PianoMotion10M dataset demonstrate that PianoFlow achieves superior quantitative and qualitative performance, while accelerating inference by over 9\times compared to previous methods.

</details>


### [77] [VideoFlexTok: Flexible-Length Coarse-to-Fine Video Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12887)
*Andrei Atanov,Jesse Allardice,Roman Bachmann,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,R Devon Hjelm,David Griffiths,Peter Fu,Afshin Dehghan,Amir Zamir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoFlexTok提出了一种从粗到细的可变长度视频token表示方法，相比传统3D网格token化，能更高效地压缩视频信息并降低下游模型学习复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频token化方法将视频表示为3D网格token，要求下游模型学习所有低层细节，无论视频固有复杂度如何，导致学习复杂度高。需要更高效的视频表示方法，能够根据视频内容自适应地组织信息。

Method: 提出VideoFlexTok，采用从粗到细的可变长度token序列表示视频：前几个token捕获抽象信息（语义和运动），后续token添加细粒度细节。使用生成流解码器实现从任意token数量的真实视频重建。

Result: 在类别和文本到视频生成任务上评估，相比3D网格token，训练效率更高：使用5倍小的模型（1.1B vs 5.2B）达到可比生成质量（gFVD和ViCLIP分数）。能够用672个token编码81帧10秒视频，比可比3D网格tokenizer少8倍。

Conclusion: VideoFlexTok提供了一种更高效、可扩展的视频表示方法，能够根据下游需求自适应token数量，支持长视频生成而无需过高计算成本，为视频生成模型提供了更好的基础表示。

Abstract: Visual tokenizers map high-dimensional raw pixels into a compressed representation for downstream modeling. Beyond compression, tokenizers dictate what information is preserved and how it is organized. A de facto standard approach to video tokenization is to represent a video as a spatiotemporal 3D grid of tokens, each capturing the corresponding local information in the original signal. This requires the downstream model that consumes the tokens, e.g., a text-to-video model, to learn to predict all low-level details "pixel-by-pixel" irrespective of the video's inherent complexity, leading to high learning complexity.
  We present VideoFlexTok, which represents videos with a variable-length sequence of tokens structured in a coarse-to-fine manner -- where the first tokens (emergently) capture abstract information, such as semantics and motion, and later tokens add fine-grained details. The generative flow decoder enables realistic video reconstructions from any token count. This representation structure allows adapting the token count according to downstream needs and encoding videos longer than the baselines with the same budget.
  We evaluate VideoFlexTok on class- and text-to-video generative tasks and show that it leads to more efficient training compared to 3D grid tokens, e.g., achieving comparable generation quality (gFVD and ViCLIP Score) with a 5x smaller model (1.1B vs 5.2B). Finally, we demonstrate how VideoFlexTok can enable long video generation without prohibitive computational cost by training a text-to-video model on 10-second 81-frame videos with only 672 tokens, 8x fewer than a comparable 3D grid tokenizer.

</details>


### [78] [Representing 3D Faces with Learnable B-Spline Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12894)
*Prashanth Chandran,Daoye Wang,Timo Bolkart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CUBE是一种新型人脸几何表示方法，结合B样条体积与学习特征，用于3D扫描配准和单目3D人脸重建，通过高维控制特征实现连续映射并保持局部编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有B样条表示使用3D控制点表达能力有限，需要更富表现力的几何表示方法，既能实现高精度3D重建，又能保持局部编辑特性，同时支持从点云和单目图像进行重建。

Method: CUBE使用高维控制特征网格（如8×8×8）定义连续两阶段映射：首先通过B样条基函数局部混合控制特征生成高维特征向量，前三个值定义基础网格；然后通过小型MLP预测残差位移，得到最终3D坐标。从固定模板网格采样查询点，实现密集语义对应。

Result: 相比现有基线方法，CUBE在3D扫描配准方面达到最先进水平，通过基于Transformer的编码器从非结构化点云和单目图像预测控制特征，验证了该表示方法的有效性。

Conclusion: CUBE成功结合了B样条表示的局部支持特性和学习特征的表达能力，为3D人脸重建和编辑提供了灵活且强大的表示框架，在保持局部编辑能力的同时实现了高质量重建。

Abstract: We present CUBE (Control-based Unified B-spline Encoding), a new geometric representation for human faces that combines B-spline volumes with learned features, and demonstrate its use as a decoder for 3D scan registration and monocular 3D face reconstruction. Unlike existing B-spline representations with 3D control points, CUBE is parametrized by a lattice (e.g., 8 x 8 x 8) of high-dimensional control features, increasing the model's expressivity. These features define a continuous, two-stage mapping from a 3D parametric domain to 3D Euclidean space via an intermediate feature space. First, high-dimensional control features are locally blended using the B-spline bases, yielding a high-dimensional feature vector whose first three values define a 3D base mesh. A small MLP then processes this feature vector to predict a residual displacement from the base shape, yielding the final refined 3D coordinates. To reconstruct 3D surfaces in dense semantic correspondence, CUBE is queried at 3D coordinates sampled from a fixed template mesh. Crucially, CUBE retains the local support property of traditional B-spline representations, enabling local surface editing by updating individual control features. We demonstrate the strengths of this representation by training transformer-based encoders to predict CUBE's control features from unstructured point clouds and monocular images, achieving state-of-the-art scan registration results compared to recent baselines.

</details>


### [79] [M3D-Stereo: A Multiple-Medium and Multiple-Degradation Dataset for Stereo Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12917)
*Deqing Yang,Yingying Liu,Qicong Wang,Zhi Zeng,Dajiang Lu,Yibin Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M3D-Stereo是一个包含7904对高分辨率立体图像的数据集，用于多介质、多退化级别的图像恢复研究，涵盖水下散射、雾霾、水下低光和雾霾低光四种退化场景，每个场景分为六个渐进退化级别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集主要局限于单一退化类型或严重依赖合成数据且缺乏立体一致性，限制了其在真实场景中的应用。需要建立一个更受控、更真实的基准来评估复杂退化环境下的图像恢复和立体匹配方法。

Method: 通过实验室设置采集数据，提供对齐的立体图像对及其像素级一致的清晰地面真值。数据集包含四种退化场景：水下散射、雾霾/雾、水下低光和雾霾低光，每个场景分为六个渐进退化级别。设计了单级退化和混合级退化两种恢复任务来验证数据集的有效性。

Result: M3D-Stereo建立了7904对高分辨率立体图像对，涵盖四种退化场景，每个场景有六个渐进退化级别。数据集提供了对齐的立体图像对和像素级一致的清晰地面真值，为图像恢复和立体匹配方法在复杂退化环境中的评估提供了更好的基准。

Conclusion: M3D-Stereo数据集通过实验室控制的多介质、多退化级别立体图像采集，为图像恢复研究提供了更受控、更真实的基准，能够支持复杂退化环境下的方法评估，并已公开在LGPLv3许可证下。

Abstract: Image restoration under adverse conditions, such as underwater, haze or fog, and low-light environments, remains a highly challenging problem due to complex physical degradations and severe information loss. Existing datasets are predominantly limited to a single degradation type or heavily rely on synthetic data without stereo consistency, inherently restricting their applicability in real-world scenarios. To address this, we introduce M3D-Stereo, a stereo dataset with 7904 high-resolution image pairs for image restoration research acquired in multiple media with multiple controlled degradation levels. It encompasses four degradation scenarios: underwater scatter, haze/fog, underwater low-light, and haze low-light. Each scenario forms a subset, and is divided into six levels of progressive degradation, allowing fine-grained evaluations of restoration methods with increasing severity of degradation. Collected via a laboratory setup, the dataset provides aligned stereo image pairs along with their pixel-wise consistent clear ground truths. Two restoration tasks, single-level and mixed-level degradation, were performed to verify its validity. M3D-Stereo establishes a better controlled and more realistic benchmark to evaluate image restoration and stereo matching methods in complex degradation environments. It is made public under LGPLv3 license.

</details>


### [80] [Radar-Camera BEV Multi-Task Learning with Cross-Task Attention Bridge for Joint 3D Detection and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12918)
*Ahmet İnanç,Özgür Erkent*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CTAB是一个双向跨任务注意力桥接模块，在BEV空间中通过多尺度可变形注意力在检测和分割分支间交换特征，实现互补信息共享，提升多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-相机融合方法将检测和分割任务孤立处理，忽略了它们之间的互补信息：检测特征编码物体级几何信息可以锐化分割边界，而分割特征提供密集语义上下文可以锚定检测。需要一种机制在共享BEV空间中实现跨任务特征交换。

Method: 提出CTAB（Cross-Task Attention Bridge）双向模块，在检测和分割分支间通过多尺度可变形注意力交换特征。集成到多任务框架中，包含基于实例归一化的分割解码器和可学习的BEV上采样，提供更详细的BEV表示。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，CTAB在保持检测性能基本不变的情况下，在7个类别上改进了分割性能。在4类子集（可行驶区域、人行横道、人行道、车辆）上，联合多任务模型达到可比的分割mIoU，同时提供3D检测能力。

Conclusion: CTAB通过跨任务特征交换有效利用了检测和分割之间的互补信息，在共享BEV空间中实现了更好的多任务性能，为自动驾驶感知提供了更统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations are the dominant paradigm for 3D perception in autonomous driving, providing a unified spatial canvas where detection and segmentation features are geometrically registered to the same physical coordinate system. However, existing radar-camera fusion methods treat these tasks in isolation, missing the opportunity to share complementary information between them: detection features encode object-level geometry that can sharpen segmentation boundaries, while segmentation features provide dense semantic context that can anchor detection. We propose \textbf{CTAB} (Cross-Task Attention Bridge), a bidirectional module that exchanges features between detection and segmentation branches via multi-scale deformable attention in shared BEV space. CTAB is integrated into a multi-task framework with an Instance Normalization-based segmentation decoder and learnable BEV upsampling to provide a more detailed BEV representation. On nuScenes, CTAB improves segmentation on 7 classes over the joint multi-task baseline at essentially neutral detection. On a 4-class subset (drivable area, pedestrian crossing, walkway, vehicle), our joint multi-task model reaches comparable mIoU on 4 classes while simultaneously providing 3D detection.

</details>


### [81] [Grasp in Gaussians: Fast Monocular Reconstruction of Dynamic Hand-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12929)
*Ayce Idil Aytekin,Xu Chen,Zhengyang Shen,Thabo Beeler,Helge Rhodin,Rishabh Dabral,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraG是一种从单目视频快速重建动态3D手-物体交互的方法，使用紧凑的高斯和表示实现高效跟踪，比现有方法快6.4倍且精度更高


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用重型神经表示优化手-物体交互重建，计算成本高且效率低。需要一种既能保持几何保真度又能实现快速跟踪的轻量级方法

Method: 1. 使用视频适应的SAM3D管道初始化物体姿态和几何，将密集高斯表示转换为轻量级SoG表示；2. 从现成的单目手部姿态初始化开始，通过2D关节和深度对齐损失细化手部运动；3. 利用紧凑的SoG表示实现高效跟踪，避免每帧细化详细3D手部外观模型

Result: 在公共基准测试中，GraG在长序列上重建时间相干的手-物体交互比先前工作快6.4倍，物体重建改进13.4%，手部关节位置误差降低超过65%

Conclusion: GraG证明了使用紧凑SoG表示结合生成式高斯初始化的有效性，能够在保持几何保真度的同时实现快速稳健的手-物体交互重建，为实时应用提供了可行方案

Abstract: We present Grasp in Gaussians (GraG), a fast and robust method for reconstructing dynamic 3D hand-object interactions from a single monocular video. Unlike recent approaches that optimize heavy neural representations, our method focuses on tracking the hand and the object efficiently, once initialized from pretrained large models. Our key insight is that accurate and temporally stable hand-object motion can be recovered using a compact Sum-of-Gaussians (SoG) representation, revived from classical tracking literature and integrated with generative Gaussian-based initializations. We initialize object pose and geometry using a video-adapted SAM3D pipeline, then convert the resulting dense Gaussian representation into a lightweight SoG via subsampling. This compact representation enables efficient and fast tracking while preserving geometric fidelity. For the hand, we adopt a complementary strategy: starting from off-the-shelf monocular hand pose initialization, we refine hand motion using simple yet effective 2D joint and depth alignment losses, avoiding per-frame refinement of a detailed 3D hand appearance model while maintaining stable articulation. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that GraG reconstructs temporally coherent hand-object interactions on long sequences 6.4x faster than prior work while improving object reconstruction by 13.4% and reducing hand's per-joint position error by over 65%.

</details>


### [82] [Distorted or Fabricated? A Survey on Hallucination in Video LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12944)
*Yiyang Huang,Yitian Zhang,Yizhou Wang,Mingyuan Zhang,Liang Shi,Huimin Zeng,Yun Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文对视频大语言模型中的幻觉问题进行了系统性综述，提出了幻觉分类框架，并总结了评估、缓解方法及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视频-语言建模取得了显著进展，但视频大语言模型中仍然存在幻觉问题，即输出看似合理但与输入视频内容相矛盾。目前缺乏对这一问题的系统性分析，阻碍了构建鲁棒可靠的视频-语言系统。

Method: 提出了一个系统性分类法，将幻觉分为动态失真和内容虚构两大核心类型，每类包含两个子类型。基于此分类法，综述了幻觉评估和缓解的最新进展，包括关键基准、指标和干预策略。分析了幻觉的根本原因，并提出了未来研究方向。

Result: 建立了全面的幻觉分类框架，识别了动态失真和内容虚构两种核心幻觉类型及其子类型。总结了现有评估基准和缓解策略，分析了幻觉的根源主要来自时间表示能力有限和视觉基础不足。提出了运动感知视觉编码器和反事实学习技术等未来方向。

Conclusion: 该综述整合了分散的研究进展，促进了对视频大语言模型中幻觉问题的系统性理解，为构建鲁棒可靠的视频-语言系统奠定了基础。通过分类框架、原因分析和未来方向，为后续研究提供了清晰的路线图。

Abstract: Despite significant progress in video-language modeling, hallucinations remain a persistent challenge in Video Large Language Models (Vid-LLMs), referring to outputs that appear plausible yet contradict the content of the input video. This survey presents a comprehensive analysis of hallucinations in Vid-LLMs and introduces a systematic taxonomy that categorizes them into two core types: dynamic distortion and content fabrication, each comprising two subtypes with representative cases. Building on this taxonomy, we review recent advances in the evaluation and mitigation of hallucinations, covering key benchmarks, metrics, and intervention strategies. We further analyze the root causes of dynamic distortion and content fabrication, which often result from limited capacity for temporal representation and insufficient visual grounding. These insights inform several promising directions for future work, including the development of motion-aware visual encoders and the integration of counterfactual learning techniques. This survey consolidates scattered progress to foster a systematic understanding of hallucinations in Vid-LLMs, laying the groundwork for building robust and reliable video-language systems. An up-to-date curated list of related works is maintained at https://github.com/hukcc/Awesome-Video-Hallucination .

</details>


### [83] [Boosting Visual Instruction Tuning with Self-Supervised Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12966)
*Sophia Sirko-Galouchenko,Monika Wysoczanska,Andrei Bursuc,Nicolas Thome,Spyros Gidaris*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出V-GIFT方法，通过将自监督学习任务转化为图像-指令-响应三元组来增强视觉指令调优，无需人工标注或架构修改，显著提升多模态大语言模型的视觉推理能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在视觉语言任务上表现良好，但在需要细粒度视觉推理的视觉中心任务上存在局限。研究发现这种局限并非源于视觉表征能力弱，而是由于指令调优过程中视觉信息利用不足，许多任务可以仅依赖语言先验部分解决

Method: 提出V-GIFT方法，将经典的自监督预训练任务（如旋转预测、颜色匹配、跨视图对应）重新表述为图像-指令-响应三元组，作为自然语言指令引入视觉指令调优。该方法无需人工标注、架构修改或额外训练阶段，仅需在训练数据中注入少量（3-10%）此类视觉基础指令

Result: 在多个模型、训练机制和基准测试中，仅注入少量视觉基础指令即可持续提升视觉中心评估的性能。实验表明该方法能有效改善多模态大语言模型的视觉推理能力

Conclusion: 通过视觉基础的自监督学习任务进行指令调优是改进多模态大语言模型视觉推理能力的有效手段，仅需对训练数据分布进行简单调整即可实现显著提升

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) perform well on many vision-language tasks but often struggle with vision-centric problems that require fine-grained visual reasoning. Recent evidence suggests that this limitation arises not from weak visual representations, but from under-utilization of visual information during instruction tuning, where many tasks can be partially solved using language priors alone. We propose a simple and lightweight approach that augments visual instruction tuning with a small number of visually grounded self-supervised tasks expressed as natural language instructions. By reformulating classical self-supervised pretext tasks, such as rotation prediction, color matching, and cross-view correspondence, as image-instruction-response triplets, we introduce supervision that cannot be solved without relying on visual evidence. Our approach requires no human annotations, no architectural modifications, and no additional training stages. Across multiple models, training regimes, and benchmarks, injecting only a small fraction (3-10%) of such visually grounded instructions consistently improves performance on vision-centric evaluations. Our findings highlight instruction tuning with visually grounded SSL tasks as a powerful lever for improving visual reasoning in MLLMs through simple adjustments to the training data distribution. Code available at: https://github.com/sirkosophia/V-GIFT

</details>


### [84] [AbdomenGen: Sequential Volume-Conditioned Diffusion Framework for Abdominal Anatomy Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12969)
*Yubraj Bhandari,Lavsen Dahal,Paul Segars,Joseph Y. Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AbdomenGen：一种用于可控腹部解剖结构生成的序列体积条件扩散框架，引入体积控制标量(VCS)实现可解释的体积调制，支持独立多器官控制，在11个腹部器官上表现出良好的几何保真度和临床实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前用于生成可控、临床意义解剖变异的计算体模系统有限，需要开发能够生成可控腹部解剖结构的方法，以支持医学成像研究和仿真研究。

Method: 提出AbdomenGen框架，采用序列体积条件扩散方法，引入体积控制标量(VCS)标准化残差来解耦器官大小与体型关系。器官掩模按顺序合成，以身体掩模和先前生成结构为条件，保持全局解剖一致性同时支持独立多器官控制。

Result: 在11个腹部器官上获得强几何保真度（如肝脏Dice系数0.83±0.05），在[-3,+3] VCS范围内实现稳定单器官校准，实现解耦多器官调制。在MERLIN的肝肿大队列中，基于Wasserstein的VCS选择将训练数据分布距离减少73.6%。

Conclusion: AbdomenGen展示了校准的、分布感知的解剖结构生成能力，适用于可控腹部体模构建和仿真研究，为医学成像研究提供了有效的可控解剖变异生成工具。

Abstract: Computational phantoms are widely used in medical imaging research, yet current systems to generate controlled, clinically meaningful anatomical variations remain limited. We present AbdomenGen, a sequential volume-conditioned diffusion framework for controllable abdominal anatomy generation. We introduce the \textbf{Volume Control Scalar (VCS)}, a standardized residual that decouples organ size from body habitus, enabling interpretable volume modulation. Organ masks are synthesized sequentially, conditioning on the body mask and previously generated structures to preserve global anatomical coherence while supporting independent, multi-organ control. Across 11 abdominal organs, the proposed framework achieves strong geometric fidelity (e.g., liver dice $0.83 \pm 0.05$), stable single-organ calibration over $[-3,+3]$ VCS, and disentangled multi-organ modulation. To showcase clinical utility with a hepatomegaly cohort selected from MERLIN, Wasserstein-based VCS selection reduces distributional distance of training data by 73.6\% . These results demonstrate calibrated, distribution-aware anatomical generation suitable for controllable abdominal phantom construction and simulation studies.

</details>


### [85] [Representation geometry shapes task performance in vision-language modeling for CT enterography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13021)
*Cristian Minoccheri,Emily Wittrup,Kayvan Najarian,Ryan Stidham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CT肠造影的视觉语言迁移学习研究：发现切片均值池化优于注意力池化用于疾病分类，多窗口RGB编码优于多平面采样，检索增强生成显著提升报告生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT肠造影是评估炎症性肠病的主要成像方式，但目前尚不清楚哪种表征选择最适合该模态的自动化分析。本研究旨在探索腹部CT肠造影的视觉语言迁移学习，为这一未充分探索的模态建立基准。

Method: 采用视觉语言迁移学习方法，比较不同池化策略（均值池化vs注意力池化）和图像编码策略（多窗口RGB编码vs多平面采样）。使用三教师伪标签框架进行无专家标注的比较，并评估检索增强生成在报告生成中的效果。

Result: 1. 均值池化在疾病分类上表现更好（59.2%三分类准确率），而注意力池化在跨模态检索上更优（0.235文本到图像MRR）。2. 多窗口RGB编码优于所有增加空间覆盖的多平面采样策略，添加冠状面和矢状面视图反而降低分类性能。3. 检索增强生成将报告生成的准确率提升7-14个百分点，将序数MAE从0.98改善到0.80-0.89。

Conclusion: 本研究为CT肠造影的视觉语言分析提供了首个基准，发现切片组织对比度比空间覆盖更重要，均值池化适合分类任务而注意力池化适合检索任务，检索增强生成能显著提升报告生成性能，为体积医学成像的视觉语言系统构建提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) enterography is a primary imaging modality for assessing inflammatory bowel disease (IBD), yet the representational choices that best support automated analysis of this modality are unknown. We present the first study of vision-language transfer learning on abdominal CT enterography and identify two main findings. First, mean pooling of slice embeddings gives better categorical disease assessment (59.2\% three-class accuracy), whereas attention pooling gives better cross-modal retrieval (0.235 text-to-image MRR). This pattern holds across all LoRA configurations tested and suggests that the two aggregators emphasize different properties of the learned representation. Second, per-slice tissue contrast matters more than broader spatial coverage: multi-window RGB encoding, which maps complementary Hounsfield Unit windows to RGB channels, outperforms all strategies that increase spatial coverage through multiplanar sampling, and in this setting adding coronal and sagittal views reduces classification performance. For report generation, fine-tuning without retrieval context yields within-1 severity accuracy at the prevalence-matched chance level (70.4\% vs.\ 71\% random), suggesting little learned ordering beyond the class distribution. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves this across all configurations, scoring 7--14 percentage points above the chance baseline and improving ordinal MAE from 0.98 to 0.80--0.89. A three-teacher pseudolabel framework enables all comparisons without expert annotations. Together, these findings provide the first baselines for this underexplored modality and offer practical guidance for building vision-language systems for volumetric medical imaging.

</details>


### [86] [Conflated Inverse Modeling to Generate Diverse and Temperature-Change Inducing Urban Vegetation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13028)
*Baris Sarper Tezcan,Hrishikesh Viswanath,Rubab Saher,Daniel Aliaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种融合正向模型与扩散生成逆向模型的框架，用于生成满足特定温度目标的多样化植被空间配置方案


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市地区因快速城市化和气候变化而日益脆弱，传统热极端监测方法（如卫星遥感和数值模型）只能从植被和城市形态预测地表温度，但逆向问题——确定能够实现期望区域温度变化的空间植被配置——尚未充分探索，且该问题本质上是欠定的，多种植被模式可能产生相似的聚合温度响应

Method: 提出融合逆向建模框架，结合预测性正向模型与基于扩散的生成逆向模型，在特定温度目标条件下生成多样化、物理合理的图像化植被模式，即使在训练数据中不存在此类组合的情况下也能实现

Result: 该框架能够在控制热结果的同时实现多样化的空间植被配置，为城市气候适应提供了可控制的逆向建模方法

Conclusion: 这项工作为城市气候适应引入了一种考虑问题固有多样性的可控逆向建模方法，代码已在GitHub仓库中提供

Abstract: Urban areas are increasingly vulnerable to thermal extremes driven by rapid urbanization and climate change. Traditionally, thermal extremes have been monitored using Earth-observing satellites and numerical modeling frameworks. For example, land surface temperature derived from Landsat or Sentinel imagery is commonly used to characterize surface heating patterns. These approaches operate as forward models, translating radiative observations or modeled boundary conditions into estimates of surface thermal states. While forward models can predict land surface temperature from vegetation and urban form, the inverse problem of determining spatial vegetation configurations that achieve a desired regional temperature shift remains largely unexplored. This task is inherently underdetermined, as multiple spatial vegetation patterns can yield similar aggregated temperature responses. Conventional regression and deterministic neural networks fail to capture this ambiguity and often produce averaged solutions, particularly under data-scarce conditions. We propose a conflated inverse modeling framework that combines a predictive forward model with a diffusion-based generative inverse model to produce diverse, physically plausible image-based vegetation patterns conditioned on specific temperature goals. Our framework maintains control over thermal outcomes while enabling diverse spatial vegetation configurations, even when such combinations are absent from training data. Altogether, this work introduces a controllable inverse modeling approach for urban climate adaptation that accounts for the inherent diversity of the problem. Code is available at the GitHub repository.

</details>


### [87] [Visual Preference Optimization with Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13029)
*Ya-Qi Yu,Fangyu Hong,Xiangyang Qu,Hao Wang,Gaojie Wu,Qiaoyu Luo,Nuo Xu,Huixin Wang,Wuheng Xu,Yongxin Liao,Zihao Chen,Haonan Li,Ziming Li,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Jihao Wu,Haoyu Ren,Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: rDPO：基于实例特定量规的多模态偏好优化框架，通过细粒度视觉推理标准提升DPO效果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖的策略外扰动或粗粒度结果信号不适合细粒度视觉推理任务，需要更精细的偏好数据来反映多模态任务的质量差异

Method: 提出rDPO框架，为每个图像-指令对创建检查表式量规，包含基本和附加标准来评分任何可能策略的响应；离线构建指令-量规池，在策略内数据构建中重复使用

Result: 在公共奖励建模基准上，量规提示显著改进30B-A3B评判器，接近GPT-5.4；在公共下游基准上，量规过滤将宏平均提升至82.69，而结果过滤降至75.82；在综合基准上，rDPO达到61.01，显著优于风格约束基线(52.36)并超越基础模型(59.48)

Conclusion: 视觉偏好优化受益于策略内数据构建与实例特定标准级反馈的结合，量规方法在多模态任务中提供更精细的质量评估

Abstract: The effectiveness of Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) depends on preference data that reflect the quality differences that matter in multimodal tasks. Existing pipelines often rely on off-policy perturbations or coarse outcome-based signals, which are not well suited to fine-grained visual reasoning. We propose rDPO, a preference optimization framework based on instance-specific rubrics. For each image-instruction pair, we create a checklist-style rubric of essential and additional criteria to score responses from any possible policies. The instruction-rubric pool is built offline and reused during the construction of on-policy data. On public reward modeling benchmarks, rubric-based prompting massively improves a 30B-A3B judge and brings it close to GPT-5.4. On public downstream benchmarks, rubric-based filtering raises the macro average to 82.69, whereas outcome-based filtering drops it to 75.82 from 81.14. When evaluating scalability on a comprehensive benchmark, rDPO achieves 61.01, markedly outperforming the style-constrained baseline (52.36) and surpassing the 59.48 base model. Together, these results show that visual preference optimization benefits from combining on-policy data construction with instance-specific criterion-level feedback.

</details>


### [88] [SceneCritic: A Symbolic Evaluator for 3D Indoor Scene Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13035)
*Kathakoli Sengupta,Kai Ao,Paola Cascante-Bonilla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneCritic是一个用于评估室内场景布局的符号化评估器，基于SceneOnto空间本体论，提供比VLM评估器更稳定、更符合人类判断的评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM和VLM生成的室内场景评估依赖于渲染视图的LLM/VLM评分器，这种评估对视角、提示词和幻觉敏感，导致评估不稳定，难以判断场景的空间合理性。

Method: 构建SceneOnto空间本体论（整合3D-FRONT、ScanNet、Visual Genome的室内场景先验），开发SceneCritic符号化评估器验证语义、方向和几何一致性；建立迭代精化测试平台，比较基于规则的碰撞约束、LLM文本布局和VLM渲染观察三种批评模式。

Result: SceneCritic比VLM评估器更符合人类判断；纯文本LLM在语义布局质量上优于VLM；基于图像的VLM精化在语义和方向校正方面最有效。

Conclusion: SceneCritic提供了稳定可靠的室内场景布局评估方法，揭示了不同批评模式在空间结构构建和修正中的有效性差异，为场景生成模型的评估和改进提供了新工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) increasingly generate indoor scenes through intermediate structures such as layouts and scene graphs, yet evaluation still relies on LLM or VLM judges that score rendered views, making judgments sensitive to viewpoint, prompt phrasing, and hallucination. When the evaluator is unstable, it becomes difficult to determine whether a model has produced a spatially plausible scene or whether the output score reflects the choice of viewpoint, rendering, or prompt. We introduce SceneCritic, a symbolic evaluator for floor-plan-level layouts. SceneCritic's constraints are grounded in SceneOnto, a structured spatial ontology we construct by aggregating indoor scene priors from 3D-FRONT, ScanNet, and Visual Genome. SceneOnto traverses this ontology to jointly verify semantic, orientation, and geometric coherence across object relationships, providing object-level and relationship-level assessments that identify specific violations and successful placements. Furthermore, we pair SceneCritic with an iterative refinement test bed that probes how models build and revise spatial structure under different critic modalities: a rule-based critic using collision constraints as feedback, an LLM critic operating on the layout as text, and a VLM critic operating on rendered observations. Through extensive experiments, we show that (a) SceneCritic aligns substantially better with human judgments than VLM-based evaluators, (b) text-only LLMs can outperform VLMs on semantic layout quality, and (c) image-based VLM refinement is the most effective critic modality for semantic and orientation correction.

</details>


### [89] [Lyra 2.0: Explorable Generative 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13036)
*Tianchang Shen,Sherwin Bahmani,Kai He,Sangeetha Grama Srinivasan,Tianshi Cao,Jiawei Ren,Ruilong Li,Zian Wang,Nicholas Sharp,Zan Gojcic,Sanja Fidler,Jiahui Huang,Huan Ling,Jun Gao,Xuanchi Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lyra 2.0是一个生成持久可探索3D世界的框架，通过解决视频生成中的空间遗忘和时间漂移问题，实现长轨迹3D一致视频生成，进而重建高质量3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在长相机轨迹、大视角变化和位置重访的复杂3D场景生成中存在空间遗忘和时间漂移问题，限制了生成3D一致视频的能力，从而影响了3D场景重建的质量。

Method: 1. 针对空间遗忘：维护每帧3D几何信息用于信息路由，检索相关历史帧并建立与目标视点的密集对应关系，依赖生成先验进行外观合成；2. 针对时间漂移：使用自增强历史数据进行训练，让模型接触自身退化的输出，学习纠正而非传播漂移。

Result: Lyra 2.0能够生成显著更长且3D一致的视频轨迹，利用这些视频微调前馈重建模型，可靠地恢复出高质量的3D场景。

Conclusion: Lyra 2.0通过解决长轨迹视频生成中的空间遗忘和时间漂移问题，实现了大规模、持久可探索3D世界的生成，将视频模型的视觉保真度和创意能力与可实时渲染的3D输出相结合。

Abstract: Recent advances in video generation enable a new paradigm for 3D scene creation: generating camera-controlled videos that simulate scene walkthroughs, then lifting them to 3D via feed-forward reconstruction techniques. This generative reconstruction approach combines the visual fidelity and creative capacity of video models with 3D outputs ready for real-time rendering and simulation. Scaling to large, complex environments requires 3D-consistent video generation over long camera trajectories with large viewpoint changes and location revisits, a setting where current video models degrade quickly. Existing methods for long-horizon generation are fundamentally limited by two forms of degradation: spatial forgetting and temporal drifting. As exploration proceeds, previously observed regions fall outside the model's temporal context, forcing the model to hallucinate structures when revisited. Meanwhile, autoregressive generation accumulates small synthesis errors over time, gradually distorting scene appearance and geometry. We present Lyra 2.0, a framework for generating persistent, explorable 3D worlds at scale. To address spatial forgetting, we maintain per-frame 3D geometry and use it solely for information routing -- retrieving relevant past frames and establishing dense correspondences with the target viewpoints -- while relying on the generative prior for appearance synthesis. To address temporal drifting, we train with self-augmented histories that expose the model to its own degraded outputs, teaching it to correct drift rather than propagate it. Together, these enable substantially longer and 3D-consistent video trajectories, which we leverage to fine-tune feed-forward reconstruction models that reliably recover high-quality 3D scenes.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [MVAdapt: Zero-Shot Multi-Vehicle Adaptation for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11854)
*Haesung Oh,Jaeheung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MVAdapt是一个物理条件化的多车辆端到端自动驾驶适应框架，通过显式地将驾驶策略与车辆物理特性关联来解决车辆领域差距问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的端到端自动驾驶模型通常在固定自车配置下训练和评估，但其驾驶策略隐式地与车辆动力学相关。当部署到具有不同尺寸、质量或传动系统特性的车辆时，性能会显著下降，这被称为车辆领域差距问题

Method: MVAdapt结合了冻结的TransFuser++场景编码器、轻量级物理编码器和交叉注意力模块，在路径点解码前将场景特征条件化于车辆属性。该框架支持零样本转移和少样本校准

Result: 在CARLA Leaderboard 1.0基准测试中，MVAdapt优于朴素转移和多体现适应基线方法，在分布内和未见车辆上都表现更好。展示了两种互补行为：对许多未见车辆的强零样本转移能力，以及对严重物理异常的高效少样本校准

Conclusion: 显式地将端到端驾驶策略条件化于车辆物理特性是实现更可转移自动驾驶模型的有效步骤。MVAdapt框架为解决车辆领域差距问题提供了有前景的解决方案

Abstract: End-to-End (E2E) autonomous driving models are usually trained and evaluated with a fixed ego-vehicle, even though their driving policy is implicitly tied to vehicle dynamics. When such a model is deployed on a vehicle with different size, mass, or drivetrain characteristics, its performance can degrade substantially; we refer to this problem as the vehicle-domain gap. To address it, we propose MVAdapt, a physics-conditioned adaptation framework for multi-vehicle E2E driving. MVAdapt combines a frozen TransFuser++ scene encoder with a lightweight physics encoder and a cross-attention module that conditions scene features on vehicle properties before waypoint decoding. In the CARLA Leaderboard 1.0 benchmark, MVAdapt improves over naive transfer and multi-embodiment adaptation baselines on both in-distribution and unseen vehicles. We further show two complementary behaviors: strong zero-shot transfer on many unseen vehicles, and data-efficient few-shot calibration for severe physical outliers. These results suggest that explicitly conditioning E2E driving policies on vehicle physics is an effective step toward more transferable autonomous driving models. All codes are available at https://github.com/hae-sung-oh/MVAdapt

</details>


### [91] [BIND-USBL: Bounding IMU Navigation Drift using USBL in Heterogeneous ASV-AUV Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11861)
*Pranav Kedia,Rajini Makam,Heiko Hamann,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIND-USBL：一种用于GPS拒止环境中AUV定位的协作框架，通过配备USBL的ASV舰队提供间歇性位置修正来限制惯性推算误差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止环境中，AUV依赖惯性推算进行定位，但传感器偏差和噪声会导致无界漂移误差。传统方法中，USBL测量精度虽高，但定位失败主要源于修正的时间稀疏性和几何可用性问题

Method: BIND-USBL框架包含三个核心组件：1）多ASV编队模型，将勘测规模和锚点布置与声学覆盖关联；2）基于冲突图的TDMA上行链路调度器，用于共享信道服务；3）接收到的USBL更新与漂移倾向的惯性推算的延迟融合

Result: 在HoloOcean模拟器中，使用异构ASV-AUV团队执行割草机式覆盖任务进行评估。结果显示：定位性能受勘测规模、声学覆盖、团队组成和ASV编队几何形状的交互影响；空间复用调度器在不违反无碰撞约束的情况下提高了每AUV的修正交付率，同时保持低端到端修正延迟

Conclusion: BIND-USBL通过系统性地解决USBL修正的时间稀疏性和几何可用性问题，有效限制了AUV惯性推算的漂移误差。该框架强调了多ASV编队几何形状和调度策略对定位性能的关键影响，为GPS拒止环境下的协作水下定位提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Accurate and continuous localization of Autonomous Underwater Vehicles (AUVs) in GPS-denied environments is a persistent challenge in marine robotics. In the absence of external position fixes, AUVs rely on inertial dead-reckoning, which accumulates unbounded drift due to sensor bias and noise. This paper presents BIND-USBL, a cooperative localization framework in which a fleet of Autonomous Surface Vessels (ASVs) equipped with Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) acoustic positioning systems provides intermittent fixes to bound AUV dead-reckoning error. The key insight is that long-duration navigation failure is driven not by the accuracy of individual USBL measurements, but by the temporal sparsity and geometric availability of those fixes. BIND-USBL combines a multi-ASV formation model linking survey scale and anchor placement to acoustic coverage, a conflict-graph-based TDMA uplink scheduler for shared-channel servicing, and delayed fusion of received USBL updates with drift-prone dead reckoning. The framework is evaluated in the HoloOcean simulator using heterogeneous ASV-AUV teams executing lawnmower coverage missions. The results show that localization performance is shaped by the interaction of survey scale, acoustic coverage, team composition, and ASV-formation geometry. Further, the spatial-reuse scheduler improves per-AUV fix delivery rate without violating the no-collision constraint, while maintaining low end-to-end fix latency.

</details>


### [92] [M2HRI: An LLM-Driven Multimodal Multi-Agent Framework for Personalized Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11975)
*Shaid Hasan,Breenice Lee,Sujan Sarker,Tariq Iqbal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M2HRI是一个基于大语言模型的多模态多智能体框架，为每个机器人配备独特个性和长期记忆，并通过协调机制处理这些差异，以提升多机器人系统中的人机交互质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人系统将机器人视为功能相同的实体，忽视了机器人个体身份对用户感知的影响，以及当存在个体差异时协调机制如何塑造多机器人行为。这限制了多机器人系统在社交环境（如家庭和医院）中的应用潜力。

Method: 提出M2HRI框架，基于大语言模型为每个机器人配备：1）可区分的个性特征；2）长期记忆系统；3）基于个体差异的集中式协调机制。通过受控用户研究（n=105）在多智能体人机交互场景中验证框架效果。

Result: 研究发现：1）LLM驱动的个性特征显著可区分并能提升交互质量；2）长期记忆改善了个性化和偏好感知；3）集中式协调显著减少了行为重叠并提升了整体交互质量。这些结果表明个体性和结构化协调对连贯且社会适宜的多智能体HRI至关重要。

Conclusion: 机器人个体身份和结构化协调机制对于实现连贯且社会适宜的多智能体人机交互至关重要。M2HRI框架通过为机器人配备个性、记忆和协调能力，为社交环境中的多机器人系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems hold significant promise for social environments such as homes and hospitals, yet existing multi-robot works treat robots as functionally identical, overlooking how robots individual identity shape user perception and how coordination shapes multi-robot behavior when such individuality is present. To address this, we introduce M2HRI, a multimodal multi-agent framework built on large language models that equips each robot with distinct personality and long-term memory, alongside a coordination mechanism conditioned on these differences. In a controlled user study (n = 105) in a multi-agent human-robot interaction (HRI) scenario, we find that LLM-driven personality traits are significantly distinguishable and enhance interaction quality, long-term memory improves personalization and preference awareness, and centralized coordination significantly reduces overlap while improving overall interaction quality. Together, these results demonstrate that both agent individuality and structured coordination are essential for coherent and socially appropriate multi-agent HRI. Project website and code are available at https://project-m2hri.github.io/.

</details>


### [93] [Bipedal-Walking-Dynamics Model on Granular Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11981)
*Xunjie Chen,Xinyan Huang,Peter Shan,Jingang Yi,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的动力学建模方法，用于捕捉和预测双足机器人在颗粒介质（如沙子）上的行走运动，通过集成动态脚-地形交互模型计算地面反作用力，并分析行走动力学和运输成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒介质（如沙子）的屈服特性会导致双足脚部显著下沉和滑动，从而引起行走运动的不确定性和不稳定性，需要开发能够准确预测这种复杂地形交互的动力学模型。

Method: 提出了一种新的动力学建模方法，集成了动态脚-地形交互模型来计算地面反作用力。该颗粒动力学模型增加了三个自由度来估计脚部下沉和滑动，这些参数对捕捉机器人行走运动学和动力学（如运输成本）至关重要。通过该模型分析了双足动力学、运输成本以及脚-地形滚动和侵入效应。

Result: 在沙地上使用双足机器人行走器进行了实验，验证了所提出的动力学模型在机器人步态轮廓、介质侵入预测和地面反作用力估计方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 这种新的动力学模型可以作为双足机器人在颗粒地形上高效行走的运动控制和优化的有力工具。

Abstract: Bipeds have demonstrated high agility and mobility in unstructured environments such as sand. The yielding of such granular media brings significant sinkage and slip of the bipedal feet, leading to uncertainty and instability of walking locomotion. We present a new dynamics-modeling approach to capture and predict bipedal-walking locomotion on granular media. A dynamic foot-terrain interaction model is integrated to compute the ground reaction force (GRF). The proposed granular dynamic model has three additional degree-of-freedom (DoF) to estimate foot sinkage and slip that are critical to capturing robot-walking kinematics and kinetics such as cost of transport (CoT). Using the new model, we analyze bipedal kinetics, CoT, and foot-terrain rolling and intrusion affects. Experiments are conducted using a biped robotic walker on sand to validate the proposed dynamic model with robot-gait profiles, media-intrusion prediction, and GRF estimations. This new dynamics model can further serve as an enabling tool for locomotion control and optimization of bipedal robots to efficiently walk on granular terrains.

</details>


### [94] [Complementarity by Construction: A Lie-Group Approach to Solving Quadratic Programs with Linear Complementarity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11991)
*Arun L. Bishop,Micah I. Reich,Zachary Manchester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Marble求解器，通过流形优化方法解决线性互补二次规划问题，特别适用于机器人学中连续动力学与离散事件混合的推理问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学中的许多问题（如操作和运动中的接触建立与断开）需要同时推理连续动力学和离散事件。这些问题可以用线性互补二次规划（LCQP）局部建模，但LCQP是非凸的，且现有求解器难以在规划管道中提供良好的局部解。

Method: 观察到互补约束在无穷小松弛下形成李群，利用此结构进行流形优化。引入数值表现良好的回缩映射，通过参数化约束使其自动满足，从而避免互补约束的经典问题。

Result: 开发了开源求解器Marble（C++实现，提供Julia和Python绑定）。在基准测试中表现有竞争力，并解决了现有方法无法收敛的多个机器人学问题。

Conclusion: 通过流形优化方法有效解决了LCQP问题，为机器人学中混合连续-离散推理问题提供了实用的求解工具，填补了现有求解器的空白。

Abstract: Many problems in robotics require reasoning over a mix of continuous dynamics and discrete events, such as making and breaking contact in manipulation and locomotion. These problems are locally well modeled by linear complementarity quadratic programs (LCQPs), an extension to QPs that introduce complementarity constraints. While very expressive, LCQPs are non-convex, and few solvers exist for computing good local solutions for use in planning pipelines. In this work, we observe that complementarity constraints form a Lie group under infinitesimal relaxation, and leverage this structure to perform on-manifold optimization. We introduce a retraction map that is numerically well behaved, and use it to parameterize the constraints so that they are satisfied by construction. The resulting solver avoids many of the classical issues with complementarity constraints. We provide an open-source solver, Marble, that is implemented in C++ with Julia and Python bindings. We demonstrate that Marble is competitive on a suite of benchmark problems, and solves a number of robotics problems where existing approaches fail to converge.

</details>


### [95] [ReefMapGS: Enabling Large-Scale Underwater Reconstruction by Closing the Loop Between Multimodal SLAM and Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11992)
*Daniel Yang,Jungseok Hong,John J. Leonard,Yogesh Girdhar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReefMapGS：基于3D高斯泼溅的增量式水下场景重建框架，无需COLMAP，通过姿态图优化SLAM获取相机位姿，实现自主水下航行器的实时3D重建


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D高斯泼溅依赖结构光运动等计算密集型方法获取相机位姿，不适合野外机器人应用。水下自主航行器具有多模态传感器数据，适合基于姿态图优化的SLAM方法，但需要将这些位姿估计与3D重建相结合

Method: 提出ReefMapGS框架：1）从高置信度区域构建初始模型；2）通过交错进行新图像观测的局部跟踪与底层3DGS场景优化来增量重建场景；3）将精化位姿集成回姿态图以全局优化整个轨迹；4）实现无需COLMAP的3D重建

Result: 在两个具有复杂几何结构的水下珊瑚礁站点实现了无需COLMAP的3D重建，并在长达700米的调查轨迹上获得了更准确的自主水下航行器全局位姿估计

Conclusion: ReefMapGS成功将3D高斯泼溅与基于姿态图优化的SLAM相结合，为野外机器人应用提供了高质量、高效的增量式3D场景重建解决方案，特别适用于水下环境

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting is a powerful visual representation, providing high-quality and efficient 3D scene reconstruction, but it is crucially dependent on accurate camera poses typically obtained from computationally intensive processes like structure-from-motion that are unsuitable for field robot applications. However, in these domains, multimodal sensor data from acoustic, inertial, pressure, and visual sensors are available and suitable for pose-graph optimization-based SLAM methods that can estimate the vehicle's trajectory and thus our needed camera poses while providing uncertainty. We propose a 3DGS-based incremental reconstruction framework, ReefMapGS, that builds an initial model from a high certainty region and progressively expands to incorporate the whole scene. We reconstruct the scene incrementally by interleaving local tracking of new image observations with optimization of the underlying 3DGS scene. These refined poses are integrated back into the pose-graph to globally optimize the whole trajectory. We show COLMAP-free 3D reconstruction of two underwater reef sites with complex geometry as well as more accurate global pose estimation of our AUV over survey trajectories spanning up to 700 m.

</details>


### [96] [A Foot Resistive Force Model for Legged Locomotion on Muddy Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12006)
*Xunjie Chen,Liuyin Wang,Xinyan Huang,Jerry Shan,Yantao Shen,Jingang Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于腿式机器人-泥泞地形交互的阻力模型，该模型捕捉了泥浆的流变特性，并基于此设计了变形机器人足部以提升在泥泞中的运动性能和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在可变形和高屈服性地形（如泥泞）上移动和导航面临重大挑战，需要准确的足-泥交互模型来指导机器人足部设计和控制策略。

Method: 提出了一个捕捉泥浆流变行为（如粘弹性、触变性和收缩吸力）的阻力模型，并利用该模型设计了一种新的变形机器人足部，通过实验验证了模型和足部设计的有效性。

Result: 实验验证表明，该阻力模型能提供准确的阻力预测，且设计的变形足部不仅增强了在泥泞中的运动机动性，还提高了行走的能源效率。

Conclusion: 新的阻力模型为腿式机器人在泥泞地形上的数据驱动仿真和运动控制开发提供了基础，变形足部设计显著改善了机器人在此类复杂地形上的性能。

Abstract: Legged robots face significant challenges in moving and navigating on deformable and highly yielding terrain such as mud. We present a resistive force model for legged foot-mud interactions. The model captures rheological behaviors such as visco-elasticity, thixotropy of the mud suspension and retractive suction. One attractive property of this new model lies in its effective, uniform formulation to provide underlying physical interpretation and accurate resistive force predictions. We further take advantage of the resistive force model to design a new morphing robotic foot for effective and efficient legged locomotion. We conduct extensive experiments to validate the force model, and the results demonstrate that the morphing foot enhances not only the locomotion mobility but also energy-efficiency of walking in mud. The new resistive force model can be further used to develop data-driven simulation and locomotion control of legged robots on muddy terrains.

</details>


### [97] [3DRO: Lidar-level SE(3) Direct Radar Odometry Using a 2D Imaging Radar and a Gyroscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12027)
*Cedric Le Gentil,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将SE(2)直接雷达里程计(DRO)扩展为SE(3)状态估计框架3DRO，通过集成3D陀螺仪测量实现三维运动估计，在643km数据上达到激光雷达级精度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管2D成像雷达提供360度环境信息，但现有方法通常假设数据局限于雷达旋转轴正交平面，仅进行SE(2)状态估计，限制了三维运动估计能力

Method: 扩展SE(2)直接雷达里程计(DRO)框架为3DRO，在保持DRO二维速度估计平面性假设基础上，集成SO(3)上的3D陀螺仪测量来估计SE(3)自运动

Result: 使用Boreas-RT数据集的643km数据进行验证，该方法实现了激光雷达级别的里程计精度，证明了简单而有效的三维状态估计能力

Conclusion: 通过将2D雷达里程计与3D陀螺仪测量相结合，3DRO框架能够实现准确的三维状态估计，为雷达感知提供了新的可能性

Abstract: Recently, the robotics community has regained interest in radar-based perception and state estimation. A 2D imaging radar provides dense 360deg information about the environment. Despite the radar antenna's cone of emission and reception, the collected data is generally assumed to be limited to the plane orthogonal to the radar's spinning axis. Accordingly, most methods based on 2D imaging radars only perform SE(2) state estimation. This paper presents 3DRO, an extension of the SE(2) Direct Radar Odometry (DRO) framework to perform state estimation in SE(3). While still assuming planarity of the data through DRO's 2D velocity estimates, it integrates 3D gyroscope measurements over SO(3) to estimate SE(3) ego motion. While simple, this approach provides lidar-level odometry accuracy as demonstrated using 643km of data from the Boreas-RT dataset.

</details>


### [98] [Dynamic Modeling and Robust Gait Optimization of a Compliant Worm Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12031)
*Xinyu Zhou,Yu Mei,Faith Thomson,Christian Luedtke,Xinda Qi,Xiaobo Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出用于柔性蠕虫机器人的实验验证建模与优化框架，通过混合动力学模型、松弛感知驱动模型和能量模型，实现多目标步态优化以平衡速度与功耗，并引入运动鲁棒性边界增强锚定转换的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性蠕虫机器人在约束环境中具有有效运动能力，但其可变形锚定结构与环境的相互作用使得预测建模和可部署步态优化变得困难。需要建立能够准确描述机器人运动行为并支持鲁棒优化的建模框架。

Method: 1) 推导混合动力学运动模型：将机器人运动表示为波纹管沟槽内的连续动力学和相邻沟槽间锚定位置的离散切换；2) 引入松弛感知驱动模型：将指令步态输入映射到实际体长变化；3) 开发基于物理原理并通过经验功率测量校准的能量模型；4) 建立多目标步态优化问题：最大化平均速度同时最小化平均功率；5) 引入运动鲁棒性边界到锚定转换条件，形成基于边界的鲁棒步态优化框架。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的框架能够捕捉机器人在测试条件下的主要运动和能耗行为，并支持实现速度-功率权衡的鲁棒步态优化。该框架能够有效处理柔性机器人与环境交互的复杂性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个实验验证的建模与优化框架，用于柔性蠕虫机器人在波纹管中的运动。通过混合动力学建模、松弛感知驱动和能量建模，结合多目标优化和鲁棒性边界，实现了对机器人运动行为的准确描述和鲁棒步态优化，为柔性机器人在约束环境中的部署提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Worm-inspired robots provide an effective locomotion strategy for constrained environments by combining cyclic body deformation with alternating anchoring. For compliant robots, however, the interaction between deformable anchoring structures and the environment makes predictive modeling and deployable gait optimization challenging. This paper presents an experimentally grounded modeling and optimization framework for a compliant worm robot capable of traversing corrugated pipes. First, a hybrid dynamic locomotion model is derived, in which the robot motion is represented by continuous dynamics within a corrugation groove and discrete switching of anchoring positions between adjacent grooves. A slack-aware actuation model is further introduced to map the commanded gait input to the realized body-length change, and an energy model is developed based on physics and calibrated with empirical power measurement. Based on these models, a multi-objective gait optimization problem is formulated to maximize average speed while minimizing average power. To reduce the fragility of nominal boundary-seeking solutions, a kinematic robustness margin is introduced into the anchoring-transition conditions, leading to a margin-based robust gait optimization framework. Experimental results show that the proposed framework captures the dominant locomotion and energy-consumption behavior of the robot over the tested conditions, and enables robust gait optimization for achieving speed-power trade-off.

</details>


### [99] [Uncertainty Guided Exploratory Trajectory Optimization for Sampling-Based Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12149)
*O. Goktug Poyrazoglu,Yukang Cao,Rahul Moorthy,Volkan Isler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UGE-TO是一种轨迹优化算法，通过不确定性引导的探索性采样实现更好的构型空间覆盖，避免局部最优解


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹优化严重依赖初始化，基于采样的方法对初始解高度敏感，在复杂环境中有限探索常导致收敛到局部最小值

Method: 将轨迹表示为不确定性椭球诱导的概率分布，通过Hellinger距离强制分布分离，在动作空间和系统动力学影响下增强轨迹多样性

Result: UGE-MPC在相同采样预算下实现更高探索和更快收敛：无障碍环境收敛快72.1%，杂乱环境收敛快66%且成功率提高6.7%

Conclusion: UGE-MPC在需要显著偏离标称轨迹以避免失败的复杂环境中表现出更高的成功率和更快的收敛速度

Abstract: Trajectory optimization depends heavily on initialization. In particular, sampling-based approaches are highly sensitive to initial solutions, and limited exploration frequently leads them to converge to local minima in complex environments. We present Uncertainty Guided Exploratory Trajectory Optimization (UGE-TO), a trajectory optimization algorithm that generates well-separated samples to achieve a better coverage of the configuration space. UGE-TO represents trajectories as probability distributions induced by uncertainty ellipsoids. Unlike sampling-based approaches that explore only in the action space, this representation captures the effects of both system dynamics and action selection. By incorporating the impact of dynamics, in addition to the action space, into our distributions, our method enhances trajectory diversity by enforcing distributional separation via the Hellinger distance between them. It enables a systematic exploration of the configuration space and improves robustness against local minima. Further, we present UGE-MPC, which integrates UGE-TO into sampling-based model predictive controller methods. Experiments demonstrate that UGE-MPC achieves higher exploration and faster convergence in trajectory optimization compared to baselines under the same sampling budget, achieving 72.1% faster convergence in obstacle-free environments and 66% faster convergence with a 6.7% higher success rate in the cluttered environment compared to the best-performing baseline. Additionally, we validate the approach through a range of simulation scenarios and real-world experiments. Our results indicate that UGE-MPC has higher success rates and faster convergence, especially in environments that demand significant deviations from nominal trajectories to avoid failures. The project and code are available at https://ogpoyrazoglu.github.io/cuniform_sampling/.

</details>


### [100] [Robotic Nanoparticle Synthesis via Solution-based Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12169)
*Dasharadhan Mahalingam,Michael Gallagher,Nilanjan Chakraborty,Stanislaus S. Wong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于螺旋几何的机器人操作规划框架，用于溶液合成自动化，通过单次演示提取运动约束，实现长时程多步骤化学实验的自主执行


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决溶液合成自动化中的核心挑战：长时程多步骤任务（如金和磁铁矿纳米颗粒制备）需要多种操作技能，其中某些技能（如倾倒、转移容器、转动旋钮）对末端执行器运动施加了几何和运动学约束，需要一种能让领域专家（化学家）轻松适应和重新编程系统的方法

Method: 采用基于螺旋几何的操纵规划框架，结合编程示教范式。从单次演示中提取恒定螺旋序列，这种表示紧凑编码运动约束且保持坐标不变性。通过组合这些螺旋参数化的基元，机器人自主生成执行完整实验的运动计划

Result: 该框架能够稳健地泛化到抓取位置的变化，并允许从单个示例中学到的技能进行参数化重用。机器人能够自主执行完整的实验方案，在重复运行中实现长时程实验室自动化

Conclusion: 螺旋理论规划与编程示教相结合，为长时程实验室自动化提供了严谨且可泛化的基础，使基础运动学能够在开发可扩展溶液合成协议的机器人应用中产生转化影响

Abstract: We present a screw geometry-based manipulation planning framework for the robotic automation of solution-based synthesis, exemplified through the preparation of gold and magnetite nanoparticles. The synthesis protocols are inherently long-horizon, multi-step tasks, requiring skills such as pick-and-place, pouring, turning a knob, and periodic visual inspection to detect reaction completion. A central challenge is that some skills, notably pouring, transferring containers with solutions, and turning a knob, impose geometric and kinematic constraints on the end-effector motion. To address this, we use a programming by demonstration paradigm where the constraints can be extracted from a single demonstration. This combination of screw-based motion representation and demonstration-driven specification enables domain experts, such as chemists, to readily adapt and reprogram the system for new experimental protocols and laboratory setups without requiring expertise in robotics or motion planning. We extract sequences of constant screws from demonstrations, which compactly encode the motion constraints while remaining coordinate-invariant. This representation enables robust generalization across variations in grasp placement and allows parameterized reuse of a skill learned from a single example. By composing these screw-parameterized primitives according to the synthesis protocol, the robot autonomously generates motion plans that execute the complete experiment over repeated runs. Our results highlight that screw-theoretic planning, combined with programming by demonstration, provides a rigorous and generalizable foundation for long-horizon laboratory automation, thereby enabling fundamental kinematics to have a translational impact on the use of robots in developing scalable solution-based synthesis protocols.

</details>


### [101] [Asymptotically Stable Gait Generation and Instantaneous Walkability Determination for Planar Almost Linear Biped with Knees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12274)
*Fumihiko Asano,Ning Lei,Taiki Sedoguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对平面双足机器人的线性化建模方法，通过泰勒展开重力项获得线性模型，实现了未来状态预测和行走能力判定的即时计算，并将该方法扩展到带膝关节的6自由度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对一类具有特殊机械特性的平面双足机器人（所有连杆围绕髋关节平衡，无法自然摆动），其运动方程具有惯性矩阵恒定、无非线性速度项的特点。通过线性化重力项，可以建立线性模型，实现无需数值积分的即时计算，但需要将这种方法扩展到更复杂的带膝关节模型。

Method: 1. 推导平面6自由度双足机器人的运动方程、约束条件和非弹性碰撞模型；2. 设计控制系统并在水平面上数值生成稳定步态；3. 将模型简化为3自由度，对大腿框架角展开点处的重力项进行线性近似，推导线性化模型；4. 通过状态空间实现和离散化，实现迭代计算的即时行走能力判定。

Result: 数值仿真表明，未来状态计算和行走能力判定可在可忽略的时间内完成。通过详细步态分析，讨论了膝关节屈曲角度和展开点对线性近似精度的影响，以及下小台阶时出现的问题。

Conclusion: 成功将线性化建模方法扩展到带膝关节的平面双足机器人，实现了高效的即时计算能力。该方法为实时行走能力判定提供了有效工具，但膝关节角度和展开点选择会影响近似精度，下台阶场景需要特别处理。

Abstract: A class of planar bipedal robots with unique mechanical properties has been proposed, where all links are balanced around the hip joint, preventing natural swinging motion due to gravity. A common property of their equations of motion is that the inertia matrix is a constant matrix, there are no nonlinear velocity terms, and the gravity term contains simple nonlinear terms. By performing a Taylor expansion of the gravity term and making a linear approximation, it is easy to derive a linearized model, and calculations for future states or walkability determination can be performed instantaneously without the need for numerical integration. This paper extends the method to a planar biped robot model with knees. First, we derive the equations of motion, constraint conditions, and inelastic collisions for a planar 6-DOF biped robot, design its control system, and numerically generate a stable bipedal gait on a horizontal plane. Next, we reduce the equations of motion to a 3-DOF model, and derive a linearized model by approximating the gravity term as linear around the expansion point for the thigh frame angle. Through numerical simulations, we demonstrate that calculations for future states and walkability determination can be completed in negligible time. By applying control inputs to the obtained model, performing state-space realization, and then discretizing it, instantaneous walkability determination through iterative calculation becomes possible. Through detailed gait analysis, we discuss how the knee joint flexion angle and the expansion point affect the accuracy of the linear approximation, and the issues that arise when descending a small step.

</details>


### [102] [Defining and Evaluation Method for External Human-Machine Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12293)
*Jose Gonzalez-Belmonte,Jaerock Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种包含223个问题的通用评估方法，用于客观评估和比较自动驾驶车辆外部人机界面（eHMI）的不同提案，并通过测试四种现有方案和基准方案验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自动驾驶车辆事故死亡人数的增加，车辆与道路其他参与者之间需要一种通用的通信方法。尽管过去十年提出了许多外部人机界面（eHMI）方案，但尚未确定理想的解决方案，因此需要一种客观的评估方法来比较不同提案。

Method: 提出了一种通用的评估方法，包含223个问题，分为7个类别：标准化便利性、成本效益、可访问性、易于理解性、通信多面性、定位和可读性。该方法应用于四种现有eHMI提案和一个仅使用运动学运动的基准方案进行测试。

Result: 测试结果表明，理想的机器-人类通信方法是精心设计的车辆运动学与分布式、布局合理的文本显示相结合。同时揭示了eHMI可读性方面的知识空白，以及不同观察者学习其含义的速度差异。

Conclusion: 本文提出的评估方法能够客观比较不同eHMI提案。理想的通信方案是运动学与文本显示的结合，但需要进一步研究eHMI的可读性和学习速度问题。建议未来围绕这些不确定性进行更多研究，并使用该评估方法进行更多测试。

Abstract: As the number of fatalities involving Autonomous Vehicles increase, the need for a universal method of communicating between vehicles and other agents on the road has also increased. Over the past decade, numerous proposals of external Human-Machine Interfaces (eHMIs) have been brought forward with the purpose of bridging this communication gap, with none yet to be determined as the ideal one. This work proposes a universal evaluation method conformed of 223 questions to objectively evaluate and compare different proposals and arrive at a conclusion. The questionnaire is divided into 7 categories that evaluate different aspects of any given proposal that uses eHMIs: ease of standardization, cost effectiveness, accessibility, ease of understanding, multifacetedness in communication, positioning, and readability. In order to test the method it was used on four existing proposals, plus a baseline using only kinematic motions, in order to both exemplify the application of the evaluation method and offer a baseline score for future comparison. The result of this testing suggests that the ideal method of machine-human communication is a combination of intentionally-designed vehicle kinematics and distributed well-placed text-based displays, but it also reveals knowledge gaps in the readability of eHMIs and the speed at which different observers may learn their meaning. This paper proposes future work related to these uncertainties, along with future testing with the proposed method.

</details>


### [103] [D-BDM: A Direct and Efficient Boundary-Based Occupancy Grid Mapping Framework for LiDARs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12436)
*Benxu Tang,Yixi Cai,Fanze Kong,Longji Yin,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D-BDM：一种高效的3D占据地图更新框架，通过截断射线投射和直接边界更新机制，显著降低计算开销和内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D占据地图存在两个根本限制：1）显式存储所有体素导致内存消耗过大；2）完全射线投射更新延迟高。现有边界表示方法虽然缓解了内存问题，但仍依赖完全射线投射更新。

Method: 提出D-BDM框架：1）截断射线投射策略，将体素遍历限制在边界外部，大幅减少更新体素数量；2）直接边界更新机制，无需辅助局部3D占据网格，简化地图更新流程。

Result: 在公开数据集上的评估表明，相比基线方法和现有边界方法，D-BDM实现了显著更低的更新时间和减少的内存消耗。

Conclusion: D-BDM通过创新的截断射线投射和直接边界更新，为自主机器人在未知环境中的高效、可扩展3D占据地图提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient and scalable 3D occupancy mapping is essential for autonomous robot applications in unknown environments. However, traditional occupancy grid representations suffer from two fundamental limitations. First, explicitly storing all voxels in three-dimensional space leads to prohibitive memory consumption. Second, exhaustive ray casting incurs high update latency. A recent representation alleviate memory demands by maintaining only the voxels on the two-dimensional boundary, yet they still rely on full ray casting updates. This work advances the boundary-based framework with a highly efficient update scheme. We introduce a truncated ray casting strategy that restricts voxel traversal to the exterior of the boundary, which dramatically reduces the number of updated voxels. In addition, we propose a direct boundary update mechanism that removes the need for an auxiliary local 3D occupancy grid, further reducing memory usage and simplifying the map update pipeline. We name our framework as D-BDM. Extensive evaluations on public datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves significantly lower update time and reduced memory consumption compared with the baseline methods, as well as the prior boundary-based approach.

</details>


### [104] [HazardArena: Evaluating Semantic Safety in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12447)
*Zixing Chen,Yifeng Gao,Li Wang,Yunhan Zhao,Yi Liu,Jiayu Li,Xiang Zheng,Zuxuan Wu,Cong Wang,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了HazardArena基准测试，用于评估视觉-语言-动作模型在语义安全方面的脆弱性，并设计了训练无关的安全选项层来减少不安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估主要关注动作执行成功率，而忽视了动作策略与视觉-语言语义的紧密耦合。这种脱节暴露了系统性脆弱性：正确的动作执行可能在语义风险下导致不安全结果。需要重新思考VLA模型在现实世界部署中的语义安全评估与保障。

Method: 1. 构建HazardArena基准：包含安全/不安全孪生场景，共享相同的对象、布局和动作要求，仅语义上下文不同；包含2000多个资产和40个风险敏感任务，涵盖7个基于机器人安全标准的现实风险类别。2. 提出训练无关的安全选项层：使用语义属性或视觉-语言判断器约束动作执行。

Result: 研究发现，仅在安全场景训练的VLA模型在对应不安全场景中经常无法安全行为。提出的安全选项层显著减少了不安全行为，同时对任务性能影响最小。

Conclusion: HazardArena揭示了VLA模型在语义安全方面的系统性脆弱性，强调了在模型向现实世界部署扩展时，需要重新思考语义安全的评估与保障方法。提出的基准和缓解方法为VLA安全研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit rich world knowledge from vision-language backbones and acquire executable skills via action demonstrations. However, existing evaluations largely focus on action execution success, leaving action policies loosely coupled with visual-linguistic semantics. This decoupling exposes a systematic vulnerability whereby correct action execution may induce unsafe outcomes under semantic risk. To expose this vulnerability, we introduce HazardArena, a benchmark designed to evaluate semantic safety in VLAs under controlled yet risk-bearing contexts. HazardArena is constructed from safe/unsafe twin scenarios that share matched objects, layouts, and action requirements, differing only in the semantic context that determines whether an action is unsafe. We find that VLA models trained exclusively on safe scenarios often fail to behave safely when evaluated in their corresponding unsafe counterparts. HazardArena includes over 2,000 assets and 40 risk-sensitive tasks spanning 7 real-world risk categories grounded in established robotic safety standards. To mitigate this vulnerability, we propose a training-free Safety Option Layer that constrains action execution using semantic attributes or a vision-language judge, substantially reducing unsafe behaviors with minimal impact on task performance. We hope that HazardArena highlights the need to rethink how semantic safety is evaluated and enforced in VLAs as they scale toward real-world deployment.

</details>


### [105] [Designing for Error Recovery in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12473)
*Christopher D. Wallbridge,Erwin Jose Lopez Pulgarin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该立场论文探讨了机器人AI系统编程方式的局限性，提出需要构建能够检测和从自身错误中恢复的系统，并以核手套箱机器人为例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前许多AI系统专注于提升单个系统性能以超越人类基准，但这些系统通常处理一次性单向决策，而现实世界是连续交互的。人类能够从错误中恢复和学习，从而实现更高的成功率。作者旨在探讨构建能够检测和从自身错误中恢复的系统的挑战。

Method: 采用立场论文的形式，通过分析当前AI系统编程方式的局限性，以核手套箱机器人作为具体用例来阐述问题，并提出初步的设计思路。

Result: 论文指出了当前AI系统在连续交互环境中的局限性，强调了错误检测和恢复能力的重要性，并通过核手套箱机器人的实际案例展示了这些挑战的具体表现。

Conclusion: 需要重新思考机器人AI系统的设计范式，从追求超越人类基准的单向性能优化转向构建能够检测、恢复并从错误中学习的系统，以适应现实世界的连续交互特性。

Abstract: This position paper looks briefly at the way we attempt to program robotic AI systems. Many AI systems are based on the idea of trying to improve the performance of one individual system to beyond so-called human baselines. However, these systems often look at one shot and one-way decisions, whereas the real world is more continuous and interactive. Humans, however, are often able to recover from and learn from errors - enabling a much higher rate of success. We look at the challenges of building a system that can detect/recover from its own errors, using the example of robotic nuclear gloveboxes as a use case to help illustrate examples. We then go on to talk about simple starting designs.

</details>


### [106] [From Kinematics to Dynamics: Learning to Refine Hybrid Plans for Physically Feasible Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12474)
*Lidor Erez,Shahaf S. Shperberg,Ayal Taitler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用强化学习将混合规划器生成的一阶轨迹优化为满足二阶动力学约束的物理可行轨迹


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人任务中的混合离散-连续规划问题：现有混合时间规划器通常使用一阶线性动力学模型，无法保证生成的轨迹满足机器人真实物理约束，导致即使高层动作序列固定，生成动态可行轨迹仍成为双层优化问题

Method: 定义显式包含解析二阶约束的马尔可夫决策过程，使用连续空间中的强化学习来优化混合规划器生成的一阶轨迹，将一阶规划与真实动力学要求桥接起来

Result: 该方法能够可靠地恢复物理可行性，有效弥合规划器初始一阶轨迹与实际执行所需动力学之间的差距

Conclusion: 通过强化学习结合二阶动力学约束，可以解决混合离散-连续规划中一阶模型无法保证物理可行性的问题，为机器人任务提供更可靠的轨迹规划方案

Abstract: In many robotic tasks, agents must traverse a sequence of spatial regions to complete a mission. Such problems are inherently mixed discrete-continuous: a high-level action sequence and a physically feasible continuous trajectory. The resulting trajectory and action sequence must also satisfy problem constraints such as deadlines, time windows, and velocity or acceleration limits. While hybrid temporal planners attempt to address this challenge, they typically model motion using linear (first-order) dynamics, which cannot guarantee that the resulting plan respects the robot's true physical constraints. Consequently, even when the high-level action sequence is fixed, producing a dynamically feasible trajectory becomes a bi-level optimization problem. We address this problem via reinforcement learning in continuous space. We define a Markov Decision Process that explicitly incorporates analytical second-order constraints and use it to refine first-order plans generated by a hybrid planner. Our results show that this approach can reliably recover physical feasibility and effectively bridge the gap between a planner's initial first-order trajectory and the dynamics required for real execution.

</details>


### [107] [Social Learning Strategies for Evolved Virtual Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12482)
*K. Ege de Bruin,Kyrre Glette,Kai Olav Ellefsen,Giorgia Nadizar,Eric Medvet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 社交学习加速机器人形态与控制联合优化：机器人可从同伴学习优化参数，相比从头学习性能更优，多教师策略效果更稳定


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人形态与控制的联合优化是耦合挑战：形态决定有效控制策略，控制参数影响形态性能。传统嵌套进化与学习方法中，每个机器人的控制参数独立学习，但一个机器人学习的参数可能对其他机器人有价值，因此需要探索社交学习方法加速优化过程。

Method: 提出社交学习框架，机器人可从同伴获取优化参数加速自身脑优化。系统研究教师选择策略（向哪些机器人学习、学习多少）对性能影响，在四个任务和环境中使用虚拟软机器人进行实验。特别研究从形态相似机器人继承经验的效果，因为机器人优化中身体与大脑紧密耦合。

Result: 社交学习在同等计算预算下明显优于从头学习，证实基于他人经验的有效性。虽然最优教师选择策略仍有待探索，但研究发现从多个教师获取知识能产生更一致和稳健的改进。

Conclusion: 社交学习能有效加速机器人形态与控制的联合优化过程，多教师策略比单一教师提供更稳定性能提升，为机器人协同进化提供新方向。

Abstract: Optimizing the body and brain of a robot is a coupled challenge: the morphology determines what control strategies are effective, while the control parameters influence how well the morphology performs. This joint optimization can be done through nested loops of evolutionary and learning processes, where the control parameters of each robot are learned independently. However, the control parameters learned by one robot may contain valuable information for others. Thus, we introduce a social learning approach in which robots can exploit optimized parameters from their peers to accelerate their own brain optimization. Within this framework, we systematically investigate how the selection of teachers, deciding which and how many robots to learn from, affects performance, experimenting with virtual soft robots in four tasks and environments. In particular, we study the effect of inheriting experience from morphologically similar robots due to the tightly coupled body and brain in robot optimization. Our results confirm the effectiveness of building on others' experience, as social learning clearly outperforms learning from scratch under equivalent computational budgets. In addition, while the optimal teacher selection strategy remains open, our findings suggest that incorporating knowledge from multiple teachers can yield more consistent and robust improvements.

</details>


### [108] [DeCoNav: Dialog enhanced Long-Horizon Collaborative Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12486)
*Sunyao Zhou,Yunzi Wu,Tianhang Wang,Xinhai Li,Guang Chen,Lizheng Liu,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeCoNav提出了一种去中心化的对话增强长视野协作视觉语言导航框架，通过事件触发对话实现动态任务分配和重规划，显著提升了多机器人协作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作视觉语言导航基准通常不强制执行共享世界时间线上的严格同步双机器人滚动，且依赖静态协调策略，无法在出现新跨智能体证据时进行自适应调整。需要一种能够实时自适应协调的框架。

Method: DeCoNav采用去中心化框架，将事件触发对话与动态任务分配和重规划相结合。机器人通过对话交换紧凑语义状态，无需中央控制器。当出现新证据、不确定性或冲突等信息性事件时，触发对话以动态重新分配子目标并在同步执行下重新规划。

Result: 在DeCoNavBench上实现，包含176个HM3D场景中的1,213个任务，DeCoNav将双成功率（BSR）提高了69.2%，证明了对话驱动、动态重新分配规划对多机器人协作的有效性。

Conclusion: DeCoNav通过事件触发对话和动态任务重分配，为长视野协作视觉语言导航提供了一种有效的去中心化自适应协调框架，显著提升了多机器人系统的协作性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon collaborative vision-language navigation (VLN) is critical for multi-robot systems to accomplish complex tasks beyond the capability of a single agent. CoNavBench takes a first step by introducing the first collaborative long-horizon VLN benchmark with relay-style multi-robot tasks, a collaboration taxonomy, along with graph-grounded generation and evaluation to model handoffs and rendezvous in shared environments. However, existing benchmarks and evaluations often do not enforce strictly synchronized dual-robot rollout on a shared world timeline, and they typically rely on static coordination policies that cannot adapt when new cross-agent evidence emerges. We present Dialog enhanced Long-Horizon Collaborative Vision-Language Navigation (DeCoNav), a decentralized framework that couples event-triggered dialogue with dynamic task allocation and replanning for real-time, adaptive coordination. In DeCoNav, robots exchange compact semantic states via dialogue without a central controller. When informative events such as new evidence, uncertainty, or conflicts arise, dialogue is triggered to dynamically reassign subgoals and replan under synchronized execution. Implemented in DeCoNavBench with 1,213 tasks across 176 HM3D scenes, DeCoNav improves the both-success rate (BSR) by 69.2%, demonstrating the effectiveness of dialogue-driven, dynamically reallocated planning for multi-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [109] [Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation using Offline Reinforcement Learning on Sub-optimal Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12509)
*Snehal Jauhri,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WHOLE-MoMa：一种两阶段管道，通过随机化轻量级全身控制器生成多样化演示，然后应用离线强化学习改进行为，实现移动机械臂对铰接物体的操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂操作铰接物体需要基座和手臂的全身协调。传统全身控制器需要大量手动调优且脆弱，而学习方法依赖昂贵的全身遥操作数据或复杂奖励工程。作者发现即使次优的全身控制器也是强大的结构先验，可用于收集任务相关数据并可通过离线强化学习改进

Method: 提出WHOLE-MoMa两阶段管道：1）通过随机化轻量级全身控制器生成多样化演示；2）应用离线强化学习通过奖励信号识别和拼接改进行为。扩展离线隐式Q学习，引入Q-chunking进行块级批评家评估和优势加权策略提取，支持表达性动作块扩散策略

Result: 在TIAGo++移动机械臂仿真中三个难度递增的任务上，WHOLE-MoMa显著优于全身控制器、行为克隆和多个离线强化学习基线。策略无需微调直接转移到真实机器人，在双手抽屉操作中达到80%成功率，在同时橱柜打开和物体放置中达到68%成功率，且无需任何遥操作或真实世界训练数据

Conclusion: WHOLE-MoMa证明了次优全身控制器作为结构先验的价值，通过结合随机化控制器和离线强化学习，实现了移动机械臂对铰接物体的复杂协调操作，且策略可直接转移到真实机器人而无需额外数据

Abstract: Mobile Manipulation (MoMa) of articulated objects, such as opening doors, drawers, and cupboards, demands simultaneous, whole-body coordination between a robot's base and arms. Classical whole-body controllers (WBCs) can solve such problems via hierarchical optimization, but require extensive hand-tuned optimization and remain brittle. Learning-based methods, on the other hand, show strong generalization capabilities but typically rely on expensive whole-body teleoperation data or heavy reward engineering. We observe that even a sub-optimal WBC is a powerful structural prior: it can be used to collect data in a constrained, task-relevant region of the state-action space, and its behavior can still be improved upon using offline reinforcement learning. Building on this, we propose WHOLE-MoMa, a two-stage pipeline that first generates diverse demonstrations by randomizing a lightweight WBC, and then applies offline RL to identify and stitch together improved behaviors via a reward signal. To support the expressive action-chunked diffusion policies needed for complex coordination tasks, we extend offline implicit Q-learning with Q-chunking for chunk-level critic evaluation and advantage-weighted policy extraction. On three tasks of increasing difficulty using a TIAGo++ mobile manipulator in simulation, WHOLE-MoMa significantly outperforms WBC, behavior cloning, and several offline RL baselines. Policies transfer directly to the real robot without finetuning, achieving 80% success in bimanual drawer manipulation and 68% in simultaneous cupboard opening and object placement, all without any teleoperated or real-world training data.

</details>


### [110] [Scalable Trajectory Generation for Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12565)
*Yida Niu,Xinhai Chang,Xin Liu,Ziyuan Jiao,Yixin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoMoMa是一个GPU加速框架，通过统一AKR建模和并行轨迹优化，实现了大规模、物理有效的协调移动操作轨迹数据生成，比CPU基线快80倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的机器人需要协调全身运动（移动基座和机械臂），但现有数据采集方法（遥操作和规划）在规模上要么劳动密集要么计算成本过高，缺乏可扩展的物理有效协调轨迹数据生成管道。

Method: AutoMoMa将基座、机械臂和物体运动学统一为单一链的AKR建模，与并行轨迹优化相结合，实现GPU加速，达到每GPU小时5000个轨迹的生成速度。
