Result: 生成超过50万条物理有效轨迹，涵盖330个场景、多样化关节物体和多种机器人本体，比CPU基线快80倍以上。下游IL策略训练显示，即使是单个关节物体任务也需要数万条演示才能达到约80%成功率。

Conclusion: AutoMoMa解决了数据稀缺这一关键瓶颈，为协调移动操作研究提供了基础设施，展示了在真实世界多样化非结构化环境中可泛化的全身机器人策略。

Abstract: Robots deployed in unstructured environments must coordinate whole-body motion -- simultaneously moving a mobile base and arm -- to interact with the physical world. This coupled mobility and dexterity yields a state space that grows combinatorially with scene and object diversity, demanding datasets far larger than those sufficient for fixed-base manipulation. Yet existing acquisition methods, including teleoperation and planning, are either labor-intensive or computationally prohibitive at scale. The core bottleneck is the lack of a scalable pipeline for generating large-scale, physically valid, coordinated trajectory data across diverse embodiments and environments. Here we introduce AutoMoMa, a GPU-accelerated framework that unifies AKR modeling, which consolidates base, arm, and object kinematics into a single chain, with parallelized trajectory optimization. AutoMoMa achieves 5,000 episodes per GPU-hour (over $80\times$ faster than CPU-based baselines), producing a dataset of over 500k physically valid trajectories spanning 330 scenes, diverse articulated objects, and multiple robot embodiments. Prior datasets were forced to compromise on scale, diversity, or kinematic fidelity; AutoMoMa addresses all three simultaneously. Training downstream IL policies further reveals that even a single articulated-object task requires tens of thousands of demonstrations for SOTA methods to reach $\approx 80\%$ success, confirming that data scarcity -- not algorithmic limitations -- has been the binding constraint. AutoMoMa thus bridges high-performance planning and reliable IL-based control, providing the infrastructure previously missing for coordinated mobile manipulation research. By making large-scale, kinematically valid training data practical, AutoMoMa showcases generalizable whole-body robot policies capable of operating in the diverse, unstructured settings of the real world.

</details>


### [111] [Machine Learning-Based Real-Time Detection of Compensatory Trunk Movements Using Trunk-Wrist Inertial Measurement Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12591)
*Jannis Gabler,Clément Lhoste,Max Quast,Laura Mayrhuber,Andrea Ronco,Olivier Lambercy,Paulius Viskaitis,Dane Donegan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用两个惯性测量单元(IMU)和机器学习实现脑卒中后代偿性躯干运动的实时检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑卒中后常见的代偿性躯干运动(CTMs)会形成不良运动模式，限制受影响结构的针对性训练。目前CTMs的客观、连续检测面临挑战，因为需要复杂的测量设置且难以实时应用。

Method: 使用两个IMU配置（手腕和躯干）采集数据，通过极端梯度提升分类器(XGBoost)进行机器学习。数据来自10名健康参与者在模拟损伤条件下执行日常生活活动，使用光学运动捕捉系统和手动视频标注作为参考。

Result: 双IMU模型表现出强大的判别性能（宏F1=0.80±0.07，MCC=0.73±0.08；ROC-AUC>0.93），性能与基于光学运动捕捉的模型相当，预测时间适合实时应用。在4名神经系统疾病患者的初步评估中，模型保持良好判别能力（ROC-AUC~0.78）。

Conclusion: 稀疏可穿戴传感是实现治疗和日常生活中CTMs可扩展、实时监测的可行途径，但在临床泛化方面仍面临挑战。

Abstract: Compensatory trunk movements (CTMs) are commonly observed after stroke and can lead to maladaptive movement patterns, limiting targeted training of affected structures. Objective, continuous detection of CTMs during therapy and activities of daily living remains challenging due to the typically complex measurements setups required, as well as limited applicability for real-time use. This study investigates whether a two-inertial measurement unit configuration enables reliable, real-time CTM detection using machine learning. Data were collected from ten able-bodied participants performing activities of daily living under simulated impairment conditions (elbow brace restricting flexion-extension, resistance band inducing flexor-synergy-like patterns), with synchronized optical motion capture (OMC) and manually annotated video recordings serving as reference. A systematic location-reduction analysis using OMC identified wrist and trunk kinematics as a minimal yet sufficient set of anatomical sensing locations. Using an extreme gradient boosting classifier (XGBoost) evaluated with leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, our two-IMU model achieved strong discriminative performance (macro-F1 = 0.80 +/- 0.07, MCC = 0.73 +/- 0.08; ROC-AUC > 0.93), with performance comparable to an OMC-based model and prediction timing suitable for real-time applications. Explainability analysis revealed dominant contributions from trunk dynamics and wrist-trunk interaction features. In preliminary evaluation using recordings from four participants with neurological conditions, the model retained good discriminative capability (ROC-AUC ~ 0.78), but showed reduced and variable threshold-dependent performance, highlighting challenges in clinical generalization. These results support sparse wearable sensing as a viable pathway toward scalable, real-time monitoring of CTMs during therapy and daily living.

</details>


### [112] [Habitat-GS: A High-Fidelity Navigation Simulator with Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12626)
*Ziyuan Xia,Jingyi Xu,Chong Cui,Yuanhong Yu,Jiazhao Zhang,Qingsong Yan,Tao Ni,Junbo Chen,Xiaowei Zhou,Hujun Bao,Ruizhen Hu,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Habitat-GS是一个基于3D高斯泼溅的具身AI仿真器，通过集成3DGS渲染和可驱动高斯化身，实现了高真实感场景渲染和动态人类建模，提升了智能体在人类密集真实场景中的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真器存在视觉真实感有限、动态人类化身建模能力不足的问题，主要依赖网格光栅化和网格表示，这限制了智能体在人类密集真实世界场景中的泛化能力。

Method: 扩展Habitat-Sim构建Habitat-GS，集成3D高斯泼溅渲染器实现实时逼真渲染，支持多源3DGS资产导入；引入高斯化身模块，使化身既能作为逼真视觉实体又能作为有效导航障碍物。

Result: 点目标导航实验表明，在3DGS场景训练的智能体具有更强的跨域泛化能力，混合域训练策略最有效；化身感知导航评估证实高斯化身支持有效的人类感知导航；性能基准测试验证了系统在不同场景复杂度和化身数量下的可扩展性。

Conclusion: Habitat-GS通过集成3D高斯泼溅渲染和高斯化身，为具身AI提供了高真实感仿真环境，显著提升了智能体在人类密集真实场景中的泛化能力和人类感知导航能力。

Abstract: Training embodied AI agents depends critically on the visual fidelity of simulation environments and the ability to model dynamic humans. Current simulators rely on mesh-based rasterization with limited visual realism, and their support for dynamic human avatars, where available, is constrained to mesh representations, hindering agent generalization to human-populated real-world scenarios. We present Habitat-GS, a navigation-centric embodied AI simulator extended from Habitat-Sim that integrates 3D Gaussian Splatting scene rendering and drivable gaussian avatars while maintaining full compatibility with the Habitat ecosystem. Our system implements a 3DGS renderer for real-time photorealistic rendering and supports scalable 3DGS asset import from diverse sources. For dynamic human modeling, we introduce a gaussian avatar module that enables each avatar to simultaneously serve as a photorealistic visual entity and an effective navigation obstacle, allowing agents to learn human-aware behaviors in realistic settings. Experiments on point-goal navigation demonstrate that agents trained on 3DGS scenes achieve stronger cross-domain generalization, with mixed-domain training being the most effective strategy. Evaluations on avatar-aware navigation further confirm that gaussian avatars enable effective human-aware navigation. Finally, performance benchmarks validate the system's scalability across varying scene complexity and avatar counts.

</details>


### [113] [Contextual Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Reef Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12645)
*Melvin Laux,Yi-Ling Liu,Rina Alo,Sören Töpper,Mariela De Lucas Alvarez,Frank Kirchner,Rebecca Adam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用上下文多任务强化学习来训练自主水下航行器的控制策略，以解决水下环境不确定性和任务变化带来的挑战，提高策略的鲁棒性和可重用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下航行器在海洋生态系统监测中具有潜力，但其部署受到高度不确定和非平稳水下动力学控制的困难所限制。传统单任务强化学习容易过拟合训练环境，限制了学习策略的长期实用性。

Method: 采用上下文多任务强化学习范式，在HoloOcean模拟的珊瑚礁环境中训练单个上下文依赖策略，该策略能够解决多个相关监测任务（如检测牡蛎和珊瑚）。

Result: 通过实验评估了上下文策略在样本效率、对未见任务的零样本泛化能力以及对变化水流的鲁棒性方面的表现。多任务强化学习提高了训练效率和所学策略的可重用性。

Conclusion: 上下文多任务强化学习能够为自主水下珊瑚礁监测学习鲁棒且可泛化的控制策略，朝着更可持续的自主监测程序迈进一步。

Abstract: Although autonomous underwater vehicles promise the capability of marine ecosystem monitoring, their deployment is fundamentally limited by the difficulty of controlling vehicles under highly uncertain and non-stationary underwater dynamics. To address these challenges, we employ a data-driven reinforcement learning approach to compensate for unknown dynamics and task variations.Traditional single-task reinforcement learning has a tendency to overfit the training environment, thus, limit the long-term usefulness of the learnt policy. Hence, we propose to use a contextual multi-task reinforcement learning paradigm instead, allowing us to learn controllers that can be reused for various tasks, e.g., detecting oysters in one reef and detecting corals in another. We evaluate whether contextual multi-task reinforcement learning can efficiently learn robust and generalisable control policies for autonomous underwater reef monitoring. We train a single context-dependent policy that is able to solve multiple related monitoring tasks in a simulated reef environment in HoloOcean. In our experiments, we empirically evaluate the contextual policies regarding sample-efficiency, zero-shot generalisation to unseen tasks, and robustness to varying water currents. By utilising multi-task reinforcement learning, we aim to improve the training effectiveness, as well as the reusability of learnt policies to take a step towards more sustainable procedures in autonomous reef monitoring.

</details>


### [114] [FeaXDrive: Feasibility-aware Trajectory-Centric Diffusion Planning for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12656)
*Baoyun Wang,Zhuoren Li,Ming Liu,Xinrui Zhang,Bo Leng,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FeaXDrive：一种面向端到端自动驾驶的可行性感知轨迹中心扩散规划方法，通过轨迹中心化建模、自适应曲率约束训练、可行驶区域引导和可行性感知GRPO后训练，显著提升生成轨迹的物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端扩散规划方法生成的轨迹存在物理可行性不足的问题，包括局部几何不规则、违反轨迹级运动学约束、偏离可行驶区域等。这表明当前基于噪声中心的扩散规划公式与轨迹空间的可行性特征不够匹配。

Method: 提出FeaXDrive方法，核心思想是将干净轨迹作为扩散过程中可行性感知建模的统一对象。基于这种轨迹中心化公式，集成：1）自适应曲率约束训练以改善内在几何和运动学可行性；2）反向扩散采样中的可行驶区域引导以增强与可行驶区域的一致性；3）可行性感知GRPO后训练以进一步提升规划性能同时平衡轨迹空间可行性。

Result: 在NAVSIM基准测试中，FeaXDrive实现了强大的闭环规划性能，同时显著提升了轨迹空间可行性。实验结果表明该方法在保持规划性能的同时有效改善了轨迹的物理可行性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在端到端扩散规划中显式建模轨迹空间可行性的重要性，为开发更可靠、物理基础更扎实的自动驾驶规划器提供了重要一步。FeaXDrive通过轨迹中心化方法有效解决了现有扩散规划中的物理可行性不足问题。

Abstract: End-to-end diffusion planning has shown strong potential for autonomous driving, but the physical feasibility of generated trajectories remains insufficiently addressed. In particular, generated trajectories may exhibit local geometric irregularities, violate trajectory-level kinematic constraints, or deviate from the drivable area, indicating that the commonly used noise-centric formulation in diffusion planning is not yet well aligned with the trajectory space where feasibility is more naturally characterized. To address this issue, we propose FeaXDrive, a feasibility-aware trajectory-centric diffusion planning method for end-to-end autonomous driving. The core idea is to treat the clean trajectory as the unified object for feasibility-aware modeling throughout the diffusion process. Built on this trajectory-centric formulation, FeaXDrive integrates adaptive curvature-constrained training to improve intrinsic geometric and kinematic feasibility, drivable-area guidance within reverse diffusion sampling to enhance consistency with the drivable area, and feasibility-aware GRPO post-training to further improve planning performance while balancing trajectory-space feasibility. Experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark show that FeaXDrive achieves strong closed-loop planning performance while substantially improving trajectory-space feasibility. These findings highlight the importance of explicitly modeling trajectory-space feasibility in end-to-end diffusion planning and provide a step toward more reliable and physically grounded autonomous driving planners.

</details>


### [115] [Reliability-Guided Depth Fusion for Glare-Resilient Navigation Costmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12753)
*Shang-En Tsai,Wei-Cheng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于深度可靠性建模的抗眩光代价地图构建方法，通过预测像素级测量可信度来减少反射表面导致的虚假障碍物


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射地板和玻璃表面的镜面眩光会破坏RGB-D深度测量，产生空洞和尖峰，这些错误测量在占用网格代价地图中累积为持久性虚假障碍物，影响机器人导航安全

Method: 提出基于显式深度可靠性建模的眩光弹性代价地图构建方法：1) 轻量级深度可靠性图(DRM)估计器预测镜面干扰下的像素级测量可信度；2) 可靠性引导融合(RGF)机制利用该信号在测量累积到地图前调制占用更新

Result: 在配备Intel RealSense D435和Jetson Orin Nano的真实移动机器人平台上实验表明，该方法在真实反射地板和玻璃表面条件下显著减少虚假障碍物插入并改善自由空间保持，同时仅引入适度的计算开销

Conclusion: 将眩光视为测量可靠性问题，为安全关键室内环境中提高代价地图正确性和导航鲁棒性提供了实用且轻量级的解决方案

Abstract: Specular glare on reflective floors and glass surfaces frequently corrupts RGB-D depth measurements, producing holes and spikes that accumulate as persistent phantom obstacles in occupancy-grid costmaps. This paper proposes a glare-resilient costmap construction method based on explicit depth-reliability modeling. A lightweight Depth Reliability Map (DRM) estimator predicts per-pixel measurement trustworthiness under specular interference, and a Reliability-Guided Fusion (RGF) mechanism uses this signal to modulate occupancy updates before corrupted measurements are accumulated into the map. Experiments on a real mobile robotic platform equipped with an Intel RealSense D435 and a Jetson Orin Nano show that the proposed method substantially reduces false obstacle insertion and improves free-space preservation under real reflective-floor and glass-surface conditions, while introducing only modest computational overhead. These results indicate that treating glare as a measurement-reliability problem provides a practical and lightweight solution for improving costmap correctness and navigation robustness in safety-critical indoor environments.

</details>


### [116] [Actuation space reduction to facilitate insightful shape matching in a novel reconfigurable tendon driven continuum manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12792)
*Sabyasachi Dash,John Golden,Girish Krishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种肌腱驱动连续体机械臂的主动可重构设计，通过旋转间隔盘改变肌腱路径，实现定制化空间变形，并基于曲率-扭率空间的模式识别开发简化顺序形状匹配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统肌腱驱动连续体机械臂的肌腱路径固定，限制了其空间变形能力。通过主动可重构的肌腱路径设计，可以实现更灵活、定制化的空间变形，但这也增加了从期望骨架曲线到执行器输入的映射复杂性。

Method: 1) 设计主动可旋转的间隔盘，可在执行前后重新配置肌腱路径；2) 将骨架形状投影到曲率-扭率中间空间，识别影响全局形状的关键间隔盘模式；3) 采用顺序形状匹配策略：先旋转近端和中间盘近似全局形状，再调整远端盘微调末端执行器位置。

Result: 该方法提供了一种无需建模的替代控制方案，绕过了可重构肌腱驱动连续体机械臂的复杂建模问题。通过曲率-扭率空间的模式识别，能够确定哪些间隔盘对实现全局形状最具影响力，从而简化控制策略。

Conclusion: 主动可重构肌腱路径设计增强了连续体机械臂的空间变形能力，而基于曲率-扭率空间模式识别的顺序形状匹配策略提供了一种有效的模型无关控制方法，简化了复杂可重构系统的控制问题。

Abstract: In tendon driven continuum manipulators (TDCMs), reconfiguring the tendon routing enables tailored spatial deformation of the backbone. This work presents a design in which tendons can be rerouted either prior to or after actuation by actively rotating the individual spacer disks. Each disk rotation thus adds a degree of freedom to the actuation space, complicating the mapping from a desired backbone curve to the corresponding actuator inputs. However, when the backbone shape is projected into an intermediate space defined by curvature and torsion (C-T), patterns emerge that highlight which disks are most influential in achieving a global shape. This insight enables a simplified, sequential shape-matching strategy: first, the proximal and intermediate disks are rotated to approximate the global shape; then, the distal disks are adjusted to fine-tune the end-effector position with minimal impact on the overall shape. The proposed actuation framework offers a model-free alternative to conventional control approaches, bypassing the complexities of modeling reconfigurable TDCMs.

</details>


### [117] [VULCAN: Vision-Language-Model Enhanced Multi-Agent Cooperative Navigation for Indoor Fire-Disaster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12831)
*Shengding Liu,Qiben Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VULCAN：基于多模态感知和视觉语言模型的多智能体协同导航框架，专为室内火灾救援设计，在模拟真实火灾场景中评估现有方法并揭示其关键失效模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾灾害由于浓烟、高温和动态变化的环境给自主搜救带来严峻挑战。现有基于视觉的多智能体导航系统主要针对良性室内环境设计，在火灾驱动的动态条件下性能显著下降，需要更鲁棒的感知和危险感知规划方法

Method: 提出VULCAN框架，基于多模态感知和视觉语言模型（VLMs），专为室内火灾灾害响应设计。扩展Habitat-Matterport3D基准，模拟物理真实的火灾场景（包括烟雾扩散、热危害和传感器退化），并评估代表性多智能体协同导航基线方法

Result: 在正常和火灾驱动环境中评估现有方法，揭示了现有方法在火灾场景中的关键失效模式，强调了鲁棒感知和危险感知规划对于可靠多智能体搜救的必要性

Conclusion: 室内火灾灾害需要专门的多智能体协同导航框架，VULCAN通过多模态感知和VLMs为这一挑战提供了解决方案，现有视觉方法在火灾条件下的性能下降凸显了开发更鲁棒感知和规划系统的紧迫性

Abstract: Indoor fire disasters pose severe challenges to autonomous search and rescue due to dense smoke, high temperatures, and dynamically evolving indoor environments. In such time-critical scenarios, multi-agent cooperative navigation is particularly useful, as it enables faster and broader exploration than single-agent approaches. However, existing multi-agent navigation systems are primarily vision-based and designed for benign indoor settings, leading to significant performance degradation under fire-driven dynamic conditions. In this paper, we present VULCAN, a multi-agent cooperative navigation framework based on multi-modal perception and vision-language models (VLMs), tailored for indoor fire disaster response. We extend the Habitat-Matterport3D benchmark by simulating physically realistic fire scenarios, including smoke diffusion, thermal hazards, and sensor degradation. We evaluate representative multi-agent cooperative navigation baselines under both normal and fire-driven environments. Our results reveal critical failure modes of existing methods in fire scenarios and underscore the necessity of robust perception and hazard-aware planning for reliable multi-agent search and rescue.

</details>


### [118] [GGD-SLAM: Monocular 3DGS SLAM Powered by Generalizable Motion Model for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12837)
*Yi Liu,Haoxuan Xu,Hongbo Duan,Keyu Fan,Zhengyang Zhang,Peiyu Zhuang,Pengting Luo,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GGD-SLAM：一种基于通用运动模型的动态环境SLAM框架，无需语义标注或深度输入，通过序列注意力机制和动态特征增强器分离静态与动态组件，在动态场景中实现最先进的定位与稠密重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于3D高斯泼溅表示的视觉SLAM算法依赖静态环境假设，在动态环境中性能显著下降。现有方法通常需要预定义的语义标注或深度输入，限制了实际应用。

Method: 1. 采用FIFO队列管理输入帧，通过序列注意力机制提取动态语义特征；2. 集成动态特征增强器分离静态与动态组件；3. 通过静态信息采样填充遮挡区域；4. 设计针对动态环境的干扰自适应SSIM损失函数。

Result: 在真实世界动态数据集上的实验表明，该系统在动态场景中的相机姿态估计和稠密重建方面达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: GGD-SLAM框架成功解决了动态环境中的SLAM挑战，无需语义标注或深度输入，通过创新的运动模型和损失函数设计显著提升了系统在动态场景中的鲁棒性和重建质量。

Abstract: Visual SLAM algorithms achieve significant improvements through the exploration of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) representations, particularly in generating high-fidelity dense maps. However, they depend on a static environment assumption and experience significant performance degradation in dynamic environments. This paper presents GGD-SLAM, a framework that employs a generalizable motion model to address the challenges of localization and dense mapping in dynamic environments - without predefined semantic annotations or depth input. Specifically, the proposed system employs a First-In-First-Out (FIFO) queue to manage incoming frames, facilitating dynamic semantic feature extraction through a sequential attention mechanism. This is integrated with a dynamic feature enhancer to separate static and dynamic components. Additionally, to minimize dynamic distractors' impact on the static components, we devise a method to fill occluded areas via static information sampling and design a distractor-adaptive Structure Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) loss tailored for dynamic environments, significantly enhancing the system's resilience. Experiments conducted on real-world dynamic datasets demonstrate that the proposed system achieves state-of-the-art performance in camera pose estimation and dense reconstruction in dynamic scenes.

</details>


### [119] [PAINT: Partner-Agnostic Intent-Aware Cooperative Transport with Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12852)
*Zhihao Cao,Tianxu An,Chenhao Li,Stelian Coros,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAINT是一个轻量级分层学习框架，用于腿部机器人协作运输，通过本体感知反馈直接推断合作伙伴意图，无需外部力/力矩传感器或负载跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作运输需要机器人在物理交互中推断合作伙伴意图，同时在复杂环境中保持稳定的运动操作。传统方法难以捕捉和建模交互信号，特别是在复杂环境中。

Method: PAINT采用分层学习框架：高层策略通过意图估计器和师生训练方案推断合作伙伴交互力矩；低层运动骨干确保鲁棒执行。该方法解耦了意图理解与地形鲁棒运动，仅使用本体感知反馈。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实世界实验表明，PAINT能在不同地形、负载和合作伙伴下实现顺从的协作运输。框架自然扩展到去中心化多机器人运输，并能通过交换底层运动骨干在不同机器人平台上迁移。

Conclusion: 负载耦合交互中的本体感知信号为合作伙伴无关的意图感知协作运输提供了可扩展接口。PAINT展示了轻量级部署的可行性，无需外部传感器或负载跟踪。

Abstract: Collaborative transport requires robots to infer partner intent through physical interaction while maintaining stable loco-manipulation. This becomes particularly challenging in complex environments, where interaction signals are difficult to capture and model. We present PAINT, a lightweight yet efficient hierarchical learning framework for partner-agonistic intent-aware collaborative legged transport that infers partner intent directly from proprioceptive feedback. PAINT decouples intent understanding from terrain-robust locomotion: A high-level policy infers the partner interaction wrench using an intent estimator and a teacher-student training scheme, while a low-level locomotion backbone ensures robust execution. This enables lightweight deployment without external force-torque sensing or payload tracking. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate compliant cooperative transport across diverse terrains, payloads, and partners. Furthermore, we show that PAINT naturally scales to decentralized multi-robot transport and transfers across robot embodiments by swapping the underlying locomotion backbone. Our results suggest that proprioceptive signals in payload-coupled interaction provide a scalable interface for partner-agnostic intent-aware collaborative transport.

</details>


### [120] [Evolving the Complete Muscle: Efficient Morphology-Control Co-design for Musculoskeletal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12855)
*Lidong Sun,Wentao Zhao,Ye Wang,Huaping Liu,Fuchun Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种肌肉骨骼系统形态与控制协同设计方法，通过引入完整的肌肉形态演化空间和谱设计演化框架，解决了传统固定生理参数模型无法适应复杂任务动态需求的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肌肉骨骼机器人研究通常依赖固定肌肉生理参数模型，这种静态物理设置无法适应复杂任务的多样化动态需求，从而限制了机器人的性能上限。需要开发能够同时优化多个生理属性的形态与控制协同设计方法。

Method: 提出Complete Musculoskeletal Morphological Evolution Space，同时演化肌肉力量、速度和刚度三个生理属性。为解决这种全面演化导致的探索空间指数级扩张问题，提出Spectral Design Evolution (SDE)高效协同优化框架，通过结合双侧对称先验和主成分分析(PCA)，将复杂肌肉参数投影到低维谱流形上，实现高效的形态探索。

Result: 在MyoSuite框架上对四个任务（行走、楼梯、丘陵和崎岖地形）进行评估，该方法相比固定形态和标准进化基线表现出更优越的学习效率和运动稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过同时演化多个肌肉生理参数并采用高效的谱设计演化框架，能够显著提升肌肉骨骼机器人在复杂地形上的学习效率和运动性能，为肌肉骨骼系统的形态与控制协同设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Musculoskeletal robots offer intrinsic compliance and flexibility, providing a promising paradigm for versatile locomotion. However, existing research typically relies on models with fixed muscle physiological parameters. This static physical setting fails to accommodate the diverse dynamic demands of complex tasks, inherently limiting the robot's performance upper bound. In this work, we focus on the morphology and control co-design of musculoskeletal systems. Unlike previous studies that optimize single physiological attributes such as stiffness, we introduce a Complete Musculoskeletal Morphological Evolution Space that simultaneously evolves muscle strength, velocity, and stiffness. To overcome the exponential expansion of the exploration space caused by this comprehensive evolution, we propose Spectral Design Evolution (SDE), a high-efficiency co-optimization framework. By integrating a bilateral symmetry prior with Principal Component Analysis (PCA), SDE projects complex muscle parameters onto a low-dimensional spectral manifold, enabling efficient morphological exploration. Evaluated on the MyoSuite framework across four tasks (Walk, Stair, Hilly, and Rough terrains), our method demonstrates superior learning efficiency and locomotion stability compared to fixed-morphology and standard evolutionary baselines.

</details>


### [121] [OVAL: Open-Vocabulary Augmented Memory Model for Lifelong Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12872)
*Jiahua Pei,Yi Liu,Guoping Pan,Yuanhao Jiang,Houde Liu,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OVAL框架，用于解决开放词汇的终身目标导航问题，通过结构化记忆描述符和基于概率的探索策略提升长期导航效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标导航方法在单次导航中表现良好，但在终身记忆表示方面存在局限性，难以在长时间内有效导航到连续目标，特别是在语义开放的任务中

Method: 提出OVAL终身开放词汇记忆框架，引入记忆描述符进行结构化记忆管理，并提出基于概率的探索策略，采用多值前沿评分提升终身探索效率

Result: 大量实验证明所提系统的高效性和鲁棒性，在长期导航任务中表现出色

Conclusion: OVAL框架通过结构化记忆管理和高效探索策略，有效解决了开放词汇终身目标导航问题，为复杂任务的长期执行提供了可行方案

Abstract: Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav) refers to an agent navigating to an object in an unseen environment, which is an ability often required in the accomplishment of complex tasks. While existing methods demonstrate proficiency in isolated single object navigation, their limitations emerge in the restricted applicability of lifelong memory representations, which ultimately hinders effective navigation toward continual targets over extended periods. To address this problem, we propose OVAL, a novel lifelong open-vocabulary memory framework, which enables efficient and precise execution of long-term navigation in semantically open tasks. Within this framework, we introduce memory descriptors to facilitate structured management of the memory model. Additionally, we propose a novel probability-based exploration strategy, utilizing a multi-value frontier scoring to enhance lifelong exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficiency and robustness of the proposed system.

</details>


### [122] [FastGrasp: Learning-based Whole-body Control method for Fast Dexterous Grasping with Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12879)
*Heng Tao,Yiming Zhong,Zemin Yang,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FastGrasp是一个基于学习的移动机器人快速抓取框架，通过两阶段强化学习策略整合抓取引导、全身控制和触觉反馈，解决了高速运动下的冲击稳定、实时全身协调和跨物体泛化等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在高速运动下的冲击稳定、实时全身协调以及跨不同物体和场景的泛化能力方面存在根本性挑战，受限于固定基座、简单夹爪或缓慢的触觉响应能力。移动机器人在物流、制造和服务应用中需要快速抓取能力。

Method: 采用两阶段强化学习策略：第一阶段通过条件变分自编码器基于物体点云生成多样化的抓取候选；第二阶段执行移动基座、机械臂和手爪的协调运动，由最优抓取选择引导。触觉感知实现实时抓取调整以处理冲击效应和物体变化。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景中展示了优越的抓取性能，通过有效的仿真到真实转移实现了跨不同物体几何形状的鲁棒操作。

Conclusion: FastGrasp框架成功解决了移动机器人快速抓取中的关键挑战，通过整合抓取引导、全身控制和触觉反馈，实现了高速、鲁棒且泛化能力强的抓取操作。

Abstract: Fast grasping is critical for mobile robots in logistics, manufacturing, and service applications. Existing methods face fundamental challenges in impact stabilization under high-speed motion, real-time whole-body coordination, and generalization across diverse objects and scenarios, limited by fixed bases, simple grippers, or slow tactile response capabilities. We propose \textbf{FastGrasp}, a learning-based framework that integrates grasp guidance, whole-body control, and tactile feedback for mobile fast grasping. Our two-stage reinforcement learning strategy first generates diverse grasp candidates via conditional variational autoencoder conditioned on object point clouds, then executes coordinated movements of mobile base, arm, and hand guided by optimal grasp selection. Tactile sensing enables real-time grasp adjustments to handle impact effects and object variations. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior grasping performance in both simulation and real-world scenarios, achieving robust manipulation across diverse object geometries through effective sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [123] [Tree Learning: A Multi-Skill Continual Learning Framework for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12909)
*Yifei Yan,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Tree Learning框架，通过根-分支层次参数继承机制解决人形机器人多技能持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，支持周期性和非周期性运动，在Unity仿真中实现100%技能保留率和高奖励性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人强化学习从单任务向多技能范式演进时，如何高效扩展新技能同时避免灾难性遗忘成为关键挑战。现有方法依赖复杂的MoE模型拓扑调整或需要训练极大模型，难以轻量部署。

Method: 提出Tree Learning框架：1) 根-分支层次参数继承机制，通过参数重用为分支技能提供运动先验；2) 结合相位调制和插值的多模态前馈适应机制，支持周期性和非周期性运动；3) 任务级奖励塑形策略加速技能收敛。

Result: Unity仿真实验表明，相比同时多任务训练，Tree Learning在各种代表性运动技能上获得更高奖励，保持100%技能保留率，实现无缝多技能切换和实时交互控制。在Super Mario风格交互场景和古典中国园林自主导航任务中验证了性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: Tree Learning通过层次参数继承有效解决了人形机器人多技能持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，实现了轻量高效的多技能学习框架，在仿真环境中展示了优异的技能保留和泛化性能。

Abstract: As reinforcement learning for humanoid robots evolves from single-task to multi-skill paradigms, efficiently expanding new skills while avoiding catastrophic forgetting has become a key challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing approaches either rely on complex topology adjustments in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models or require training extremely large-scale models, making lightweight deployment difficult. To address this, we propose Tree Learning, a multi-skill continual learning framework for humanoid robots. The framework adopts a root-branch hierarchical parameter inheritance mechanism, providing motion priors for branch skills through parameter reuse to fundamentally prevent catastrophic forgetting. A multi-modal feedforward adaptation mechanism combining phase modulation and interpolation is designed to support both periodic and aperiodic motions. A task-level reward shaping strategy is also proposed to accelerate skill convergence. Unity-based simulation experiments show that, in contrast to simultaneous multi-task training, Tree Learning achieves higher rewards across various representative locomotion skills while maintaining a 100% skill retention rate, enabling seamless multi-skill switching and real-time interactive control. We further validate the performance and generalization capability of Tree Learning on two distinct Unity-simulated tasks: a Super Mario-inspired interactive scenario and autonomous navigation in a classical Chinese garden environment.

</details>


### [124] [E2E-Fly: An Integrated Training-to-Deployment System for End-to-End Quadrotor Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12916)
*Fangyu Sun,Fanxing Li,Linzuo Zhang,Yu Hu,Renbiao Jin,Shuyu Wu,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E2E-Fly：首个将可微分物理学习与训练、验证和真实世界部署系统化统一的四旋翼无人机端到端控制框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决四旋翼无人机从仿真到真实世界策略迁移的挑战，包括视觉渲染效率低、物理建模不准确、传感器差异未建模以及缺乏集成可微分物理学习的统一平台，现有系统缺乏系统化的零样本迁移流程

Method: 提出E2E-Fly集成框架，包含敏捷四旋翼平台和全栈训练-验证-部署流程。训练框架采用支持可微分物理学习和强化学习的高性能仿真器，结合针对四旋翼任务的奖励设计。采用两阶段验证策略（仿真到仿真转移和硬件在环测试），并通过专用低级控制接口和全面的仿真到真实对齐方法部署到两个物理平台

Result: 成功训练六个端到端控制任务并在真实世界部署，证明了框架的有效性。这是首个系统化统一可微分物理学习与训练、验证和真实世界部署的四旋翼无人机工作

Conclusion: E2E-Fly框架成功解决了四旋翼无人机从仿真到真实世界的端到端控制策略迁移问题，通过集成可微分物理学习、系统化验证流程和全面的仿真到真实对齐方法，实现了高效、可复现的真实世界部署

Abstract: Training and transferring learning-based policies for quadrotors from simulation to reality remains challenging due to inefficient visual rendering, physical modeling inaccuracies, unmodeled sensor discrepancies, and the absence of a unified platform integrating differentiable physics learning into end-to-end training. While recent work has demonstrated various end-to-end quadrotor control tasks, few systems provide a systematic, zero-shot transfer pipeline, hindering reproducibility and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we introduce E2E-Fly, an integrated framework featuring an agile quadrotor platform coupled with a full-stack training, validation, and deployment workflow. The training framework incorporates a high-performance simulator with support for differentiable physics learning and reinforcement learning, alongside structured reward design tailored to common quadrotor tasks. We further introduce a two-stage validation strategy using sim-to-sim transfer and hardware-in-the-loop testing, and deploy policies onto two physical quadrotor platforms via a dedicated low-level control interface and a comprehensive sim-to-real alignment methodology, encompassing system identification, domain randomization, latency compensation, and noise modeling. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically unify differentiable physical learning with training, validation, and real-world deployment for quadrotors. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework for training six end-to-end control tasks and deploy them in the real world.

</details>


### [125] [DINO-Explorer: Active Underwater Discovery via Ego-Motion Compensated Semantic Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12933)
*Yuhan Jin,Nayari Marie Lessa,Mariela De Lucas Alvarez,Melvin Laux,Lucas Amparo Barbosa,Frank Kirchner,Rebecca Adam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DINO-Explorer：基于DINOv3基础模型的主动感知框架，通过语义惊喜信号实现水下自主机器人对科学价值事件的在线检测与带宽高效传输


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数自主水下航行器作为被动数据记录器运行，捕获大量视频进行离线审查，经常错过具有高科学价值的瞬态事件。需要从被动感知转向主动感知，需要一个因果的在线信号来突出显著现象，同时抑制机动引起的视觉变化。

Method: 提出DINO-Explorer框架：在冻结的DINOv3基础模型的潜在空间中操作，利用轻量级、动作条件化的递归预测器来预测短期语义演化。采用受传出副本启发的模块，利用全局池化的光流来抵消自诱导的视觉变化，而不抑制真实的环境新颖性。

Result: 系统在固定操作点下保留了78.8%的后发现人类审查共识事件，触发确认率为56.8%。自我运动条件化相对于未补偿的惊喜信号基线抑制了45.5%的误报。在回放侧帕累托消融研究中，DINO-Explorer在选定操作点将遥测带宽减少了48.2%，同时保持了62.2%的峰值F1分数。

Conclusion: DINO-Explorer提供了一个鲁棒、带宽高效的注意力机制，成功地将数据传输集中在人类验证的新颖事件周围，为水下监测中的主动感知提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Marine ecosystem degradation necessitates continuous, scientifically selective underwater monitoring. However, most autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) operate as passive data loggers, capturing exhaustive video for offline review and frequently missing transient events of high scientific value. Transitioning to active perception requires a causal, online signal that highlights significant phenomena while suppressing maneuver-induced visual changes. We propose DINO-Explorer, a novelty-aware perception framework driven by a continuous semantic surprise signal. Operating within the latent space of a frozen DINOv3 foundation model, it leverages a lightweight, action-conditioned recurrent predictor to anticipate short-horizon semantic evolution. An efference-copy-inspired module utilizes globally pooled optical flow to discount self-induced visual changes without suppressing genuine environmental novelty. We evaluate this signal on the downstream task of asynchronous event triage under variant telemetry constraints. Results demonstrate that DINO-Explorer provides a robust, bandwidth-efficient attention mechanism. At a fixed operating point, the system retains 78.8% of post-discovery human-reviewer consensus events with a 56.8% trigger confirmation rate, effectively surfacing mission-relevant phenomena. Crucially, ego-motion conditioning suppresses 45.5% of false positives relative to an uncompensated surprise signal baseline. In a replay-side Pareto ablation study, DINO-Explorer robustly dominates the validated peak F1 versus telemetry bandwidth frontier, reducing telemetry bandwidth by 48.2% at the selected operating point while maintaining a 62.2% peak F1 score, successfully concentrating data transmission around human-verified novelty events.

</details>


### [126] [RMGS-SLAM: Real-time Multi-sensor Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12942)
*Dongen Li,Yi Liu,Junqi Liu,Zewen Sun,Zefan Huang,Shuo Sun,Jiahui Liu,Chengran Yuan,Hongliang Guo,Francis E. H. Tay,Marcelo H. Ang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种紧密耦合的LiDAR-惯性-视觉3D高斯溅射SLAM框架，用于大规模真实场景的实时位姿估计和逼真建图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS SLAM方法在大规模真实环境中难以同时实现低延迟位姿估计、与传感器流同步的3D高斯重建以及长期全局一致性

Method: 采用并行执行状态估计和3D高斯基元初始化的架构，结合前馈预测与体素PCA几何先验的级联策略，并基于优化后的全局高斯地图进行闭环检测

Result: 在公开数据集和自建数据集上的实验表明，该方法在实时效率、定位精度和渲染质量之间实现了良好平衡

Conclusion: 提出的LIV 3DGS SLAM框架能够在大规模真实场景中实现实时位姿估计和逼真建图，解决了现有方法的局限性

Abstract: Real-time 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS)-based Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) in large-scale real-world environments remains challenging, as existing methods often struggle to jointly achieve low-latency pose estimation, 3D Gaussian reconstruction in step with incoming sensor streams, and long-term global consistency. In this paper, we present a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual (LIV) 3DGS-based SLAM framework for real-time pose estimation and photorealistic mapping in large-scale real-world scenes. The system executes state estimation and 3D Gaussian primitive initialization in parallel with global Gaussian optimization, thereby enabling continuous dense mapping. To improve Gaussian initialization quality and accelerate optimization convergence, we introduce a cascaded strategy that combines feed-forward predictions with voxel-based principal component analysis (voxel-PCA) geometric priors. To enhance global consistency in large scenes, we further perform loop closure directly on the optimized global Gaussian map by estimating loop constraints through Gaussian-based Generalized Iterative Closest Point (GICP) registration, followed by pose-graph optimization. In addition, we collected challenging large-scale looped outdoor SLAM sequences with hardware-synchronized LiDAR-camera-IMU and ground-truth trajectories to support realistic and comprehensive evaluation. Extensive experiments on both public datasets and our dataset demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a strong balance among real-time efficiency, localization accuracy, and rendering quality across diverse and challenging real-world scenes.

</details>


### [127] [XRZero-G0: Pushing the Frontier of Dexterous Robotic Manipulation with Interfaces, Quality and Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13001)
*Junming Wang,Teng Pu,Wingmun Fung,Jindong Wang,Shanchang Wang,Yuan Deng,Shuyuan Wang,Ziwei Liu,Kunhao Pan,Ping Yang,Peng Zhai,Yuxin Liang,Xiaofan Li,Jiabi Sun,Renchao Xu,Xiaotian Tian,Pengfei Yan,Guoqiang Ye,Liang Li,Qian Wang,Ruyi Gan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XRZero-G0系统通过硬件-软件协同设计，结合VR接口和专用夹具，实现高效机器人操作数据收集，通过闭环质量控制和数据混合策略，大幅降低数据采集成本，实现跨具身零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作基础模型面临高质量、动作对齐演示数据的获取瓶颈。虽然无机器人的人类演示（如UMI范式）提供了比传统遥操作更可扩展的替代方案，但现有系统存在硬件人机工程学不佳、开环工作流程以及缺乏系统性数据混合策略等限制。

Method: 提出XRZero-G0硬件-软件协同设计系统：1）配备顶视摄像头和双专用夹具的VR人机工程学接口提高收集效率；2）针对非本体感知数据的闭环收集、检查、训练和评估管道确保数据集可靠性；3）研究无机器人数据的经验扩展行为和最优混合比例。

Result: 系统实现85%数据有效性率，建立透明质量控制机制。实验表明，少量真实机器人数据与大规模无机器人数据混合（如10:1比例）能达到与纯真实机器人数据集相当的性能，同时将采集成本降低20倍。构建了2000小时无机器人数据集，实现到目标物理机器人的零样本跨具身迁移。

Conclusion: XRZero-G0系统提供了一种高度可扩展的方法论，通过硬件-软件协同设计、闭环质量控制和优化数据混合策略，显著降低了高质量机器人操作数据的获取成本，实现了有效的跨具身知识迁移，为通用真实世界操作奠定了基础。

Abstract: The acquisition of high-quality, action-aligned demonstration data remains a fundamental bottleneck in scaling foundation models for dexterous robot manipulation. Although robot-free human demonstrations (e.g., the UMI paradigm) offer a scalable alternative to traditional teleoperation, current systems are constrained by sub-optimal hardware ergonomics, open-loop workflows, and a lack of systematic data-mixing strategies. To address these limitations, we present XRZero-G0, a hardware-software co-designed system for embodied data collection and policy learning. The system features an ergonomic, virtual reality interface equipped with a top-view camera and dual specialized grippers to directly improve collection efficiency. To ensure dataset reliability, we propose a closed-loop collection, inspection, training, and evaluation pipeline for non-proprioceptive data. This workflow achieves an 85% data validity rate and establishes a transparent mechanism for quality control. Furthermore, we investigate the empirical scaling behaviors and optimal mixing ratios of robot-free data. Extensive experiments indicate that combining a minimal volume of real-robot data with large-scale robot-free data (e.g., a 10:1 ratio) achieves performance comparable to exclusively real-robot datasets, while reducing acquisition costs by a factor of twenty. Utilizing XRZero-G0, we construct a 2,000-hour robot-free dataset that enables zero-shot cross-embodiment transfer to a target physical robot, demonstrating a highly scalable methodology for generalized real-world manipulation.Our project repository: https://github.com/X-Square-Robot/XRZero-G0

</details>


### [128] [Learning Versatile Humanoid Manipulation with Touch Dreaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13015)
*Yaru Niu,Zhenlong Fang,Binghong Chen,Shuai Zhou,Revanth Senthilkumaran,Hao Zhang,Bingqing Chen,Chen Qiu,H. Eric Tseng,Jonathan Francis,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HTD方法，结合强化学习全身控制器、VR遥操作数据收集和触觉预测Transformer，实现高灵巧度人形机器人操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人在现实世界中的运动操作挑战，需要全身稳定性、灵巧手部操作和接触感知能力

Method: 1) 基于RL的全身控制器保证稳定执行；2) VR遥操作结合人体-人形映射的数据收集系统；3) HTD多模态Transformer，通过触觉预测学习接触感知表示

Result: 在五个接触丰富的任务中，HTD相比最强基线平均成功率提升90.9%；潜在空间触觉预测比原始触觉预测效果更好，带来30%相对增益

Conclusion: 结合鲁棒全身执行、可扩展数据收集和预测性触觉中心学习，可实现现实世界中多功能高灵巧度人形机器人操作

Abstract: Humanoid robots promise general-purpose assistance, yet real-world humanoid loco-manipulation remains challenging because it requires whole-body stability, dexterous hands, and contact-aware perception under frequent contact changes. In this work, we study dexterous, contact-rich humanoid loco-manipulation. We first develop an RL-based whole-body controller that provides stable lower-body and torso execution during complex manipulation. Built on this controller, we develop a whole-body humanoid data collection system that combines VR-based teleoperation with human-to-humanoid motion mapping, enabling efficient collection of real-world demonstrations. We then propose Humanoid Transformer with Touch Dreaming (HTD), a multimodal encoder--decoder Transformer that models touch as a core modality alongside multi-view vision and proprioception. HTD is trained in a single stage with behavioral cloning augmented by touch dreaming: in addition to predicting action chunks, the policy predicts future hand-joint forces and future tactile latents, encouraging the shared Transformer trunk to learn contact-aware representations for dexterous interaction. Across five contact-rich tasks, Insert-T, Book Organization, Towel Folding, Cat Litter Scooping, and Tea Serving, HTD achieves a 90.9% relative improvement in average success rate over the stronger baseline. Ablation results further show that latent-space tactile prediction is more effective than raw tactile prediction, yielding a 30% relative gain in success rate. These results demonstrate that combining robust whole-body execution, scalable humanoid data collection, and predictive touch-centered learning enables versatile, high-dexterity humanoid manipulation in the real world. Project webpage: humanoid-touch-dream.github.io.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Can AI Detect Life? Lessons from Artificial Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11915)
*Ankit Gupta,Christoph Adami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI生命检测方法易被分布外样本欺骗，在非生命样本上产生接近100%的假阳性检测结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习方法被提议用于检测地外生命样本，但需要验证这些方法在分布外样本上的可靠性，因为地外样本很可能与地球生物/非生物样本分布不同

Method: 使用人工生命（Artificial Life）技术，分析机器学习方法在分布外样本上的表现，评估其被欺骗产生假阳性检测结果的可能性

Result: 机器学习方法容易被分布外样本欺骗，即使分析样本不具备生命能力，也能以接近100%的置信度被检测为生命

Conclusion: 由于地外样本很可能与训练数据分布不同，使用AI方法进行生命检测必然会产生显著的假阳性结果，需要谨慎对待

Abstract: Modern machine learning methods have been proposed to detect life in extraterrestrial samples, drawing on their ability to distinguish biotic from abiotic samples based on training models using natural and synthetic organic molecular mixtures. Here we show using Artificial Life that such methods are easily fooled into detecting life with near 100% confidence even if the analyzed sample is not capable of life. This is due to modern machine learning methods' propensity to be easily fooled by out-of-distribution samples. Because extra-terrestrial samples are very likely out of the distribution provided by terrestrial biotic and abiotic samples, using AI methods for life detection is bound to yield significant false positives.

</details>


### [130] [Interpretable DNA Sequence Classification via Dynamic Feature Generation in Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12060)
*Nicolas Huynh,Krzysztof Kacprzyk,Ryan Sheridan,David Bentley,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DEFT框架利用大语言模型在决策树构建过程中自适应生成高层次序列特征，解决传统轴对齐决策树在DNA序列分析中表达性不足和树深度过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在DNA序列分析中表现出色但缺乏可解释性，而传统轴对齐决策树虽然可解释，但受限于在每次分裂时仅考虑孤立原始特征，导致表达能力不足、树深度过大，从而影响可解释性和泛化性能。

Method: DEFT框架在树构建过程中自适应生成高层次序列特征，利用大语言模型提出针对每个节点局部序列分布的生物学信息特征，并通过反思机制迭代优化这些特征。

Result: 实验证明DEFT能够在多种基因组任务中发现人类可解释且具有高预测性的序列特征。

Conclusion: DEFT通过结合大语言模型的生物学知识和自适应特征生成，在保持可解释性的同时提高了DNA序列分析的预测性能，解决了传统可解释方法表达性不足的问题。

Abstract: The analysis of DNA sequences has become critical in numerous fields, from evolutionary biology to understanding gene regulation and disease mechanisms. While deep neural networks can achieve remarkable predictive performance, they typically operate as black boxes. Contrasting these black boxes, axis-aligned decision trees offer a promising direction for interpretable DNA sequence analysis, yet they suffer from a fundamental limitation: considering individual raw features in isolation at each split limits their expressivity, which results in prohibitive tree depths that hinder both interpretability and generalization performance. We address this challenge by introducing DEFT, a novel framework that adaptively generates high-level sequence features during tree construction. DEFT leverages large language models to propose biologically-informed features tailored to the local sequence distributions at each node and to iteratively refine them with a reflection mechanism. Empirically, we demonstrate that DEFT discovers human-interpretable and highly predictive sequence features across a diverse range of genomic tasks.

</details>


### [131] [Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning with LLMs for Generalizable Multimodal Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11835)
*Hongxi Mao,Wei Zhou,Mengting Jia,Tao Fang,Huan Gao,Bin Zhang,Shangyang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning方法，利用LLM将结构化变量转换为语义自然语言陈述，实现跨未见模式的零样本对齐，在痴呆症诊断任务中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据的机器学习受限于模式泛化能力差，特别是在临床医学等模式差异显著的领域。现有方法缺乏对结构化变量的语义理解，导致跨不同电子健康记录模式的迁移能力不足。

Method: 提出Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning方法：1）将结构化变量转换为语义自然语言陈述；2）使用预训练LLM编码这些陈述；3）实现跨未见模式的零样本对齐，无需手动特征工程或重新训练；4）将编码器集成到多模态框架中，结合表格和MRI数据进行痴呆症诊断。

Result: 在NACC和ADNI数据集上的实验显示：1）达到最先进的性能；2）成功实现跨未见模式的零样本迁移；3）在回顾性诊断任务中显著优于临床基线方法，包括委员会认证的神经科医生。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的方法为异构真实世界数据提供了可扩展、鲁棒的解决方案，为将LLM推理扩展到结构化领域开辟了途径，特别是在临床医学等模式变化显著的领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Machine learning for tabular data remains constrained by poor schema generalization, a challenge rooted in the lack of semantic understanding of structured variables. This challenge is particularly acute in domains like clinical medicine, where electronic health record (EHR) schemas vary significantly. To solve this problem, we propose Schema-Adaptive Tabular Representation Learning, a novel method that leverages large language models (LLMs) to create transferable tabular embeddings. By transforming structured variables into semantic natural language statements and encoding them with a pretrained LLM, our approach enables zero-shot alignment across unseen schemas without manual feature engineering or retraining. We integrate our encoder into a multimodal framework for dementia diagnosis, combining tabular and MRI data. Experiments on NACC and ADNI datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance and successful zero-shot transfer to unseen schemas, significantly outperforming clinical baselines, including board-certified neurologists, in retrospective diagnostic tasks. These results validate our LLM-driven approach as a scalable, robust solution for heterogeneous real-world data, offering a pathway to extend LLM-based reasoning to structured domains.

</details>


### [132] [When Reasoning Models Hurt Behavioral Simulation: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11840)
*Sandro Andric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究发现，在模拟有限理性行为时，增强推理能力的语言模型可能成为更好的"求解器"但更差的"模拟器"，导致过度优化和妥协行为消失，而有限反思能产生更真实的行为多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究普遍假设更强的推理能力会提高语言模型在社交、经济和政策模拟中的保真度。但本文质疑这一假设，指出当目标是模拟有限理性行为而非解决战略问题时，推理增强的模型可能产生"求解器-采样器不匹配"问题。

Method: 在三个多智能体谈判环境中研究求解器-采样器不匹配：模糊碎片化权威交易限制场景、模糊统一反对交易限制场景，以及新的电网削减应急管理案例。比较三种反思条件（无反思、有限反思、原生推理），在两个主要模型家族上进行测试，并将相同协议扩展到GPT-4.1和GPT-5.2的直接运行。

Result: 在所有三个实验中，有限反思比无反思或原生推理产生更多样化和妥协导向的轨迹。在OpenAI扩展中，GPT-5.2原生推理在45次运行中全部以权威决策结束，而GPT-5.2有限反思在每个环境中都恢复了妥协结果。

Conclusion: 模型能力和模拟保真度是不同的目标，行为模拟应将模型视为"采样器"而不仅仅是"求解器"。有限反思能更好地模拟有限理性行为，而过度推理可能导致不真实的战略优化。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agents in social, economic, and policy simulations. A common assumption is that stronger reasoning should improve simulation fidelity. We argue that this assumption can fail when the objective is not to solve a strategic problem, but to sample plausible boundedly rational behavior. In such settings, reasoning-enhanced models can become better solvers and worse simulators: they can over-optimize for strategically dominant actions, collapse compromise-oriented terminal behavior, and sometimes exhibit a diversity-without-fidelity pattern in which local variation survives without outcome-level fidelity. We study this solver-sampler mismatch in three multi-agent negotiation environments adapted from earlier simulation work: an ambiguous fragmented-authority trading-limits scenario, an ambiguous unified-opposition trading-limits scenario, and a new-domain grid-curtailment case in emergency electricity management. We compare three reflection conditions, no reflection, bounded reflection, and native reasoning, across two primary model families and then extend the same protocol to direct OpenAI runs with GPT-4.1 and GPT-5.2. Across all three experiments, bounded reflection produces substantially more diverse and compromise-oriented trajectories than either no reflection or native reasoning. In the direct OpenAI extension, GPT-5.2 native ends in authority decisions in 45 of 45 runs across the three experiments, while GPT-5.2 bounded recovers compromise outcomes in every environment. The contribution is not a claim that reasoning is generally harmful. It is a methodological warning: model capability and simulation fidelity are different objectives, and behavioral simulation should qualify models as samplers, not only as solvers.

</details>


### [133] [DBGL: Decay-aware Bipartite Graph Learning for Irregular Medical Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11842)
*Jian Chen,Yuzhu Hu,Xiaoyan Yuan,Yuxuan Hu,Jinfeng Xu,Yipeng Du,Wenhao Yuan,Wei Wang,Edith C. H. Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DBGL提出了一种用于不规则医学时间序列的衰减感知二分图学习方法，通过患者-变量二分图建模不规则采样模式，并设计节点特定的时间衰减编码机制来捕捉变量衰减不规则性，在四个公开数据集上优于所有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学时间序列数据具有固有的不规则性，包括异构采样率、异步观测和可变间隔等挑战。现有方法往往扭曲时间采样不规则性和缺失模式，同时无法捕捉变量衰减不规则性，导致次优表示。

Method: DBGL首先构建患者-变量二分图，同时捕捉不规则采样模式而无需人工对齐，并自适应建模变量关系以增强表示学习。为建模变量衰减不规则性，设计了节点特定的时间衰减编码机制，基于采样间隔捕捉每个变量的衰减率。

Result: 在四个公开可用的数据集上评估DBGL性能，结果显示DBGL在所有基线方法中表现最优。

Conclusion: DBGL通过同时建模时间采样不规则性和变量衰减不规则性，为不规则医学时间序列提供了更准确和可靠的表示学习方法。

Abstract: Irregular Medical Time Series play a critical role in the clinical domain to better understand the patient's condition. However, inherent irregularity arising from heterogeneous sampling rates, asynchronous observations, and variable gaps poses key challenges for reliable modeling. Existing methods often distort temporal sampling irregularity and missingness patterns while failing to capture variable decay irregularity, resulting in suboptimal representations. To address these limitations, we introduce DBGL, Decay-Aware Bipartite Graph Learning for Irregular Medical Time Series. DBGL first introduces a patient-variable bipartite graph that simultaneously captures irregular sampling patterns without artificial alignment and adaptively models variable relationships for temporal sampling irregularity modeling, enhancing representation learning. To model variable decay irregularity, DBGL designs a novel node-specific temporal decay encoding mechanism that captures each variable's decay rates based on sampling interval, yielding a more accurate and faithful representation of irregular temporal dynamics. We evaluate the performance of DBGL on four publicly available datasets, and the results show that DBGL outperforms all baselines.

</details>


### [134] [Disposition Distillation at Small Scale: A Three-Arc Negative Result](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11867)
*Hari Sadasivan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文报告了一个负面结果：尝试通过蒸馏、注意力干预和侧车架构将行为倾向（自我验证、不确定性承认、反馈整合）注入小型语言模型（0.6B-2.3B参数）均告失败，未能提升模型表现反而损害内容质量或导致风格模仿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索是否能够通过系统化的方法将特定的行为倾向（如自我验证、不确定性承认、反馈整合）注入小型语言模型中，以提升其在评估任务中的表现。初始的内部草稿报告了显著的正向结果，但后续验证发现这些结果是虚假的，这促使研究者进行更严谨的验证并探索失败的原因。

Method: 采用四阶段全MIT蒸馏流程，随后进行三个主要实验弧：1）在三个模型家族和两个领域上进行SFT/DPO LoRA微调；2）在推理时对o_proj注意力头进行温度调节干预；3）使用训练冻结的基础模型作为侧车，读取最终token的隐藏状态h_last。研究在五个不同的小型模型上进行测试，并进行了分布内交叉验证和新鲜提示测试。

Result: 所有尝试均失败：没有发现任何操作能在不损害内容质量或不陷入风格模仿的情况下，通过评估者测量来改变模型的行为倾向。分布内交叉验证（AUC=0.683）在新提示上崩溃至随机水平（AUC=0.516）。Gemma 4 E2B在Chef领域表现出近乎完全的置信度-正确性解耦（断言不对称性-0.009）。

Conclusion: 研究贡献了一个包含机制的三弧负面结果、一个针对线性h_last探针的双失败模式分类法，以及一个能够将类似研究者自身产生的假阳性结果转化为可发表负面结果的诚实证伪流程。研究发现将行为倾向注入小型语言模型具有根本性挑战，并揭示了现有评估方法的局限性。

Abstract: We set out to train behavioral dispositions (self-verification, uncertainty acknowledgment, feedback integration) into small language models (0.6B to 2.3B effective parameters) through a four-stage all-MIT distillation pipeline, with follow-on experiments on inference-time attention-head interventions and a frozen-base confidence-gated sidecar. An internal draft reported +33.9-point MCAS and +15.3-point HumanEval gains on a Qwen3-0.6B student; a second-pass sanity check falsified both numbers before publication. The HumanEval delta was a truncation artifact (n_predict=512) that inverted to -8.0 points at n_predict=1024; the MCAS gain disappeared under apples-to-apples scoring. That falsification triggered three subsequent arcs. Across (1) SFT/DPO LoRA on three model families and two domains, (2) inference-time attention-head tempering on o_proj, and (3) a training-free frozen-base sidecar reading the final-token hidden state h_last, we find no operator that moves judge-measured disposition without damaging content or collapsing into stylistic mimicry. The failure is consistent across five models (Qwen3-0.6B, Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3.5-0.8B, Gemma 4 E2B, and SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct). A within-distribution cross-validation pass (AUC=0.683) collapsed to chance on fresh prompts (AUC=0.516). We contribute a three-arc negative result with mechanism, a two-failure-mode taxonomy for linear h_last probes, and an honest falsification pipeline that converts the class of false positives we ourselves produced into publishable negatives. As an independent finding, Gemma 4 E2B exhibits near-complete confidence-correctness decoupling on the Chef domain (assertion asymmetry -0.009; the model asserts at 91% regardless of correctness).

</details>


### [135] [Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Networks: Physics-Bounded Deep Learning for Solar Forecasting in Autonomous Off-Grid Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11909)
*Mohammed Ezzaldin Babiker Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network，通过将气象和几何变量投影到Koopman线性化黎曼流形，解决光伏预测中的时间相位滞后和夜间虚假发电问题，实现零滞后同步和严格的天体几何约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型在自主离网光伏系统预测中存在严重问题：云层瞬变时的时间相位滞后和物理上不可能的夜间发电。需要解决数据驱动建模与确定性天体力学之间的分歧。

Method: 提出Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network，将22个气象和几何变量投影到Koopman线性化黎曼流形，系统映射复杂气候动态。架构包含光谱校准单元和乘法热力学Alpha门，合成实时大气不透明度与理论晴空边界模型，强制实施严格的天体几何合规性。

Result: 在五年半干旱气候测试中，RMSE为18.31 Wh/m²，Pearson相关系数0.988。严格保持所有1826个测试日夜间误差为零，高频光学瞬变时相位响应低于30分钟。模型仅含63,458个可训练参数。

Conclusion: 该超轻量设计建立了热力学一致的标准，可用于边缘部署的微电网控制器，完全消除夜间虚假发电，并在快速天气变化时保持零滞后同步。

Abstract: The stable operation of autonomous off-grid photovoltaic systems requires solar forecasting algorithms that respect atmospheric thermodynamics. Contemporary deep learning models consistently exhibit critical anomalies, primarily severe temporal phase lags during cloud transients and physically impossible nocturnal power generation. To resolve this divergence between data-driven modeling and deterministic celestial mechanics, this research introduces the Thermodynamic Liquid Manifold Network. The methodology projects 22 meteorological and geometric variables into a Koopman-linearized Riemannian manifold to systematically map complex climatic dynamics. The architecture integrates a Spectral Calibration unit and a multiplicative Thermodynamic Alpha-Gate. This system synthesizes real-time atmospheric opacity with theoretical clear-sky boundary models, structurally enforcing strict celestial geometry compliance. This completely neutralizes phantom nocturnal generation while maintaining zero-lag synchronization during rapid weather shifts. Validated against a rigorous five-year testing horizon in a severe semi-arid climate, the framework achieves an RMSE of 18.31 Wh/m2 and a Pearson correlation of 0.988. The model strictly maintains a zero-magnitude nocturnal error across all 1826 testing days and exhibits a sub-30-minute phase response during high-frequency optical transients. Comprising exactly 63,458 trainable parameters, this ultra-lightweight design establishes a robust, thermodynamically consistent standard for edge-deployable microgrid controllers.

</details>


### [136] [How Transformers Learn to Plan via Multi-Token Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11912)
*Jianhao Huang,Zhanpeng Zhou,Renqiu Xia,Baharan Mirzasoleiman,Weijie Su,Wei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多令牌预测（MTP）在推理任务中优于传统的下一令牌预测（NTP），通过梯度解耦机制实现反向推理过程，提升模型对全局结构的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统下一令牌预测（NTP）在推理任务中难以捕捉全局结构，而新兴的多令牌预测（MTP）虽然表现出潜力，但其工作机制尚不明确。本文旨在深入理解MTP如何促进推理，特别是规划任务。

Method: 采用实证与理论相结合的方法：1）在合成图路径查找任务和实际推理基准（如Countdown和布尔可满足性问题）上比较MTP与NTP性能；2）在星图任务上分析简化的两层Transformer，理论证明MTP诱导的反向推理机制及其梯度解耦特性。

Result: 实证结果显示MTP在各类推理任务上持续优于NTP。理论分析表明MTP诱导两阶段反向推理过程：模型先关注终点节点，然后逆向追踪中间节点重构路径。这种机制源于MTP的梯度解耦特性，相比NTP提供更清晰的训练信号。

Conclusion: 多令牌目标函数通过梯度解耦机制，使优化过程偏向于构建鲁棒且可解释的推理电路，从而在推理任务中实现更优性能。这揭示了MTP在促进结构化推理方面的内在优势。

Abstract: While next-token prediction (NTP) has been the standard objective for training language models, it often struggles to capture global structure in reasoning tasks. Multi-token prediction (MTP) has recently emerged as a promising alternative, yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this paper, we study how MTP facilitates reasoning, with a focus on planning. Empirically, we show that MTP consistently outperforms NTP on both synthetic graph path-finding tasks and more realistic reasoning benchmarks, such as Countdown and boolean satisfiability problems. Theoretically, we analyze a simplified two-layer Transformer on a star graph task. We prove that MTP induces a two-stage reverse reasoning process: the model first attends to the end node and then reconstructs the path by tracing intermediate nodes backward. This behavior arises from a gradient decoupling property of MTP, which provides a cleaner training signal compared to NTP. Ultimately, our results highlight how multi-token objectives inherently bias optimization toward robust and interpretable reasoning circuits.

</details>


### [137] [INTARG: Informed Real-Time Adversarial Attack Generation for Time-Series Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11928)
*Gamze Kirman Tokgoz,Onat Gungor,Tajana Rosing,Baris Aksanli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对时间序列预测的在线有界缓冲区对抗攻击框架，通过选择性攻击高置信度且预期误差最大的时间步，实现更少但更有效的攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测在许多实际系统中至关重要，但基于机器学习和深度学习的预测模型容易受到对抗攻击。现有攻击方法在时间序列场景中不适用，因为存储完整历史数据或在每个时间步进行攻击通常不切实际

Method: 提出在线有界缓冲区设置下的对抗攻击框架，采用知情选择性攻击策略。通过选择性地攻击模型置信度高且预期预测误差最大的时间步，减少攻击次数但提高攻击效果

Result: 实验表明，该框架能将预测误差提高至2.42倍，同时在少于10%的时间步进行攻击，证明了攻击的有效性和效率

Conclusion: 该研究提出的选择性对抗攻击框架在时间序列预测中既有效又实用，为理解时间序列预测模型的脆弱性和开发防御机制提供了重要见解

Abstract: Time-series forecasting aims to predict future values by modeling temporal dependencies in historical observations. It is a critical component of many real-world systems, where accurate forecasts improve operational efficiency and help mitigate uncertainty and risk. More recently, machine learning (ML), and especially deep learning (DL)-based models, have gained widespread adoption for time-series forecasting, but they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks. However, many state-of-the-art attack methods are not directly applicable in time-series settings, where storing complete historical data or performing attacks at every time step is often impractical. This paper proposes an adversarial attack framework for time-series forecasting under an online bounded-buffer setting, leveraging an informed and selective attack strategy. By selectively targeting time steps where the model exhibits high confidence and the expected prediction error is maximal, our framework produces fewer but substantially more effective attacks. Experiments show that our framework can increase the prediction error up to 2.42x, while performing attacks in fewer than 10% of time steps.

</details>


### [138] [Fast and principled equation discovery from chaos to climate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11929)
*Yuzheng Zhang,Weizhen Li,Rui Carvalho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Bayesian-ARGOS：结合快速频率筛选与聚焦贝叶斯推断的混合框架，实现自动化方程发现与不确定性量化，计算成本显著降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于库的稀疏回归方法在自动化、统计严谨性和计算效率之间存在权衡，需要一种能够同时满足这些需求的方程发现方法

Method: 开发Bayesian-ARGOS混合框架，结合快速频率筛选（用于快速候选方程识别）和聚焦贝叶斯推断（用于精确不确定性量化），提供统计诊断工具如影响分析和多重共线性检测

Result: 在7个混沌系统测试中，在数据稀缺和噪声条件下优于SINDy和bootstrap-based ARGOS，计算成本降低两个数量级，与SINDy-SHRED结合时提高了有效潜在方程的产量和长期稳定性

Conclusion: Bayesian-ARGOS提供了从稀缺噪声观测到可解释控制方程的严谨、自动化且计算高效的途径，适用于从基准混沌系统到全球气候模式潜在动力学的方程发现

Abstract: Our ability to predict, control, and ultimately understand complex systems rests on discovering the equations that govern their dynamics. Identifying these equations directly from noisy, limited observations has therefore become a central challenge in data-driven science, yet existing library-based sparse regression methods force a compromise between automation, statistical rigor, and computational efficiency. Here we develop Bayesian-ARGOS, a hybrid framework that reconciles these demands by combining rapid frequentist screening with focused Bayesian inference, enabling automated equation discovery with principled uncertainty quantification at a fraction of the computational cost of existing methods. Tested on seven chaotic systems under varying data scarcity and noise levels, Bayesian-ARGOS outperforms two state-of-the-art methods in most scenarios. It surpasses SINDy in data efficiency for all systems and noise tolerance for six out of the seven, with a two-order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost compared to bootstrap-based ARGOS. The probabilistic formulation additionally enables a suite of standard statistical diagnostics, including influence analysis and multicollinearity detection that expose failure modes otherwise opaque. When integrated with representation learning (SINDy-SHRED) for high dimensional sea surface temperature reconstruction, Bayesian-ARGOS increases the yield of valid latent equations with significantly improved long horizon stability. Bayesian-ARGOS thus provides a principled, automated, and computationally efficient route from scarce and noisy observations to interpretable governing equations, offering a practical framework for equation discovery across scales, from benchmark chaotic systems to the latent dynamics underlying global climate patterns.

</details>


### [139] [Active Imitation Learning for Thermal- and Kernel-Aware LFM Inference on 3D S-NUCA Many-Cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11948)
*Yixian Shen,Chaoyao Shen,Jan Deen,George Floros,Andy Pimentel,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AILFM：基于主动模仿学习的调度框架，用于在3D S-NUCA CPU系统上优化大型基础模型推理的热管理和性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GPU运行大型基础模型成本高且资源有限，3D S-NUCA CPU系统虽提供更高带宽和局部性，但面临严重热挑战和不均匀缓存延迟，现有热管理方法过于简化且缺乏适应性

Method: 提出AILFM框架，采用主动模仿学习方法，从Oracle演示中学习近最优的热感知调度策略，考虑核心级性能异构性和LFM内核特定行为，实现最小运行时开销

Result: AILFM在广泛实验中优于最先进的基线方法，并在多样化的LFM工作负载上表现出良好的泛化能力

Conclusion: AILFM为3D S-NUCA CPU系统上的大型基础模型推理提供了有效的热感知调度解决方案，平衡了热安全和性能最大化

Abstract: Large Foundation Model (LFM) inference is both memory- and compute-intensive, traditionally relying on GPUs. However, the limited availability and high cost have motivated the adoption of high-performance general-purpose CPUs, especially emerging 3D-stacked Static Non-Uniform Cache Architecture (3D S-NUCA) systems. These architectures offer enhanced bandwidth and locality but suffer from severe thermal challenges and uneven cache latencies due to 3D Networks-on-Chip (NoC). Optimal management of thread migration and V/f scaling is non-trivial due to LFM kernel diversity and system heterogeneity. Existing thermal management approaches often rely on oversimplified analytical models and lack adaptability. We propose AILFM, an Active Imitation Learning (AIL)-based scheduling framework that learns near-optimal thermal-aware scheduling policies from Oracle demonstrations with minimal run-time overhead. AILFM accounts for both core-level performance heterogeneity and kernel-specific behavior in LFMs to maintain thermal safety while maximizing performance. Extensive experiments show that AILFM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines and generalizes well across diverse LFM workloads.

</details>


### [140] [Classification of Epileptic iEEG using Topological Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11971)
*Sunia Tanweer,Narayan Puthanmadam Subramaniyam,Firas A. Khasawneh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索拓扑数据分析(TDA)特征在癫痫发作检测中的应用，通过大规模消融实验发现降维后的拓扑表示能达到80%的平衡准确率，且经典机器学习模型与深度学习模型表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫发作检测面临脑电信号高维度和非线性动态特性的挑战，需要探索拓扑数据分析特征是否能改善脑状态分类，特别是在多通道iEEG数据中。

Method: 分析55名患者的多通道iEEG数据，从信号中提取持久图，使用Carlsson坐标、持久图像和模板函数等多种TDA表示进行向量化。进行大规模消融研究，涵盖多个iEEG频段、降维技术、特征表示和分类器架构。

Result: 降维后的拓扑表示在三类分类中达到80%的平衡准确率。经典机器学习模型表现与深度学习模型相当（79.17%平衡准确率），表明精心设计的拓扑特征能显著降低模型复杂度需求。保留完整多通道特征结构的流水线因高维特征空间而出现严重过拟合。

Conclusion: 将拓扑表示应用于多通道神经数据时，结构保持的降维至关重要。拓扑特征能有效捕捉癫痫脑电信号的动态特性，且经典机器学习模型在适当降维后能取得与深度学习相当的性能。

Abstract: Epileptic seizure detection from EEG signals remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and nonlinear, potentially stochastic, dynamics of neural activity. In this work, we investigate whether features derived from topological data analysis (TDA) can improve the classification of brain states in preictal, ictal and interictal iEEG recordings from epilepsy patients using multichannel data. We analyze data from 55 patients, significantly larger than many previous studies that rely on patient-specific models. Persistence diagrams derived from iEEG signals are vectorized using several TDA representations, including Carlsson coordinates, persistence images, and template functions. To understand how topological representations interact with modern machine learning pipelines, we conduct a large-scale ablation study across multiple iEEG frequency bands, dimensionality reduction techniques, feature representations, and classifier architectures. Our experiments show that dimension-reduced topological representations achieve up to 80\% balanced accuracy for three-class classification. Interestingly, classical machine learning models perform comparably to deep learning models, achieving up to 79.17\% balanced accuracy, suggesting that carefully designed topological features can substantially reduce model complexity requirements. In contrast, pipelines preserving the full multichannel feature structure exhibit severe overfitting due to the high-dimensional feature space. These findings highlight the importance of structure-preserving dimensionality reduction when applying topology-based representations to multichannel neural data.

</details>


### [141] [Multi-Head Residual-Gated DeepONet for Coherent Nonlinear Wave Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11972)
*Zhiwei Fan,Yiming Pan,Daniel Coca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MH-RG DeepONet，一种结合并行状态路径和物理描述符条件路径的神经算子架构，通过多头残差门控机制实现物理感知的波动力学建模


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经算子将输入输出映射视为黑盒高维回归问题，未能充分利用波动力学中初始状态的紧凑物理描述符结构。现有特征集成策略通常依赖直接拼接或FiLM风格仿射调制，缺乏对物理上下文的显式利用

Method: 提出多头残差门控DeepONet：1) 标准DeepONet状态路径学习波场；2) 并行条件路径处理紧凑物理描述符；3) 预分支残差调制器、分支残差门和主干残差门构成三重门控；4) 低秩多头机制捕获多个互补条件响应模式，避免参数爆炸

Result: 在高度非线性保守波动力学和耗散俘获动力学基准测试中，相比特征增强基线，MH-RG DeepONet实现更低误差，更好保持相位相干性和物理相关动力学量的保真度

Conclusion: MH-RG DeepONet通过显式分离状态学习和物理描述符条件路径，结合多头残差门控机制，为物理感知的神经算子设计提供了新范式，在波动力学建模中表现出优越性能

Abstract: Coherent nonlinear wave dynamics are often strongly shaped by a compact set of physically meaningful descriptors of the initial state. Traditional neural operators typically treat the input-output mapping as a largely black-box high-dimensional regression problem, without explicitly exploiting this structured physical context. Common feature-integration strategies usually rely on direct concatenation or FiLM-style affine modulation in hidden latent spaces. Here we introduce a different paradigm, loosely inspired by the complementary roles of state evolution and physically meaningful observables in quantum mechanics: the wave field is learned through a standard DeepONet state pathway, while compact physical descriptors follow a parallel conditioning pathway and act as residual modulation factors on the state prediction. Based on this idea, we develop a Multi-Head Residual-Gated DeepONet (MH-RG), which combines a pre-branch residual modulator, a branch residual gate, and a trunk residual gate with a low-rank multi-head mechanism to capture multiple complementary conditioned response patterns without prohibitive parameter growth. We evaluate the framework on representative benchmarks including highly nonlinear conservative wave dynamics and dissipative trapped dynamics and further perform detailed mechanistic analyses of the learned multi-head gating behavior. Compared with feature-augmented baselines, MH-RG DeepONet achieves consistently lower error while better preserving phase coherence and the fidelity of physically relevant dynamical quantities.

</details>


### [142] [Offline-Online Reinforcement Learning for Linear Mixture MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11994)
*Zhongjun Zhang,Sean R. Sinclair*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究离线-在线强化学习在环境偏移下的线性混合MDPs中，提出自适应利用离线数据的算法，在离线数据有益时提升性能，无益时安全忽略，并建立理论界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究离线-在线强化学习在环境偏移下的问题，即离线数据可能来自不匹配的环境，需要自适应地利用这些数据来提升在线学习性能。

Method: 提出自适应算法，根据离线数据的质量（覆盖度或环境偏移大小）动态决定如何利用离线数据。当离线数据信息丰富时利用它们，否则安全忽略并匹配纯在线学习性能。

Result: 建立了遗憾上界，明确刻画离线数据何时有益，并提供了近乎匹配的下界。数值实验进一步验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该算法能够自适应地利用离线数据，在离线数据有益时提升性能，无益时安全回退到纯在线学习，为环境偏移下的离线-在线强化学习提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study offline-online reinforcement learning in linear mixture Markov decision processes (MDPs) under environment shift. In the offline phase, data are collected by an unknown behavior policy and may come from a mismatched environment, while in the online phase the learner interacts with the target environment. We propose an algorithm that adaptively leverages offline data. When the offline data are informative, either due to sufficient coverage or small environment shift, the algorithm provably improves over purely online learning. When the offline data are uninformative, it safely ignores them and matches the online-only performance. We establish regret upper bounds that explicitly characterize when offline data are beneficial, together with nearly matching lower bounds. Numerical experiments further corroborate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [143] [Loss-Driven Bayesian Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11995)
*Zhuoyue Huang,Freddie Bickford Smith,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于损失的贝叶斯主动学习方法，通过将数据采集直接针对特定决策问题的损失函数，推导出最优数据采集目标，并在加权Bregman散度形式的损失下实现解析计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动学习方法在针对不同下游问题和损失函数定制数据采集方面灵活性有限，需要一种能够直接针对特定决策问题损失进行数据采集的严格方法。

Method: 提出损失驱动的贝叶斯主动学习方法，从任意损失函数推导出最优数据采集目标，特别证明对于加权Bregman散度形式的损失，其目标的关键组成部分可以解析计算。

Result: 在回归和分类实验中，使用多种不同损失函数，该方法相比现有技术显著降低了测试损失。

Conclusion: 该方法为主动学习提供了一种严格且实用的损失驱动框架，能够针对特定决策问题的损失函数定制数据采集策略，在多种损失设置下优于现有方法。

Abstract: The central goal of active learning is to gather data that maximises downstream predictive performance, but popular approaches have limited flexibility in customising this data acquisition to different downstream problems and losses. We propose a rigorous loss-driven approach to Bayesian active learning that allows data acquisition to directly target the loss associated with a given decision problem. In particular, we show how any loss can be used to derive a unique objective for optimal data acquisition. Critically, we then show that any loss taking the form of a weighted Bregman divergence permits analytic computation of a central component of its corresponding objective, making the approach applicable in practice. In regression and classification experiments with a range of different losses, we find our approach reduces test losses relative to existing techniques.

</details>


### [144] [BayMOTH: Bayesian optiMizatiOn with meTa-lookahead -- a simple approacH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12005)
*Rahman Ejaz,Varchas Gopalaswamy,Ricardo Luna,Aarne Lees,Vineet Gundecha,Christopher Kanan,Soumyendu Sarkar,Riccardo Betti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应元贝叶斯优化算法，在任务相关性高时利用元训练信息，相关性低时回退到前瞻策略，实现鲁棒性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统元贝叶斯优化在任务结构匹配时样本效率高，但当元训练任务与测试任务对齐不佳时，会导致在线优化过程中提出次优查询。需要一种能自适应利用相关任务信息的方法

Method: 提出简单的元贝叶斯优化算法，在确定有用时利用相关任务信息，否则回退到前瞻策略，形成统一框架

Result: 在函数优化任务上与现有方法具有竞争力，同时在任务相关性低的场景下（测试任务与元训练集共享有限结构）保持强大性能

Conclusion: 提出的自适应元贝叶斯优化框架能够灵活利用相关任务信息，在任务相关性高时提高样本效率，在相关性低时避免性能下降，实现鲁棒的优化性能

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) has for sequential optimization of expensive black-box functions demonstrated practicality and effectiveness in many real-world settings. Meta-Bayesian optimization (meta-BO) focuses on improving the sample efficiency of BO by making use of information from related tasks. Although meta-BO is sample-efficient when task structure transfers, poor alignment between meta-training and test tasks can cause suboptimal queries to be suggested during online optimization. To this end, we propose a simple meta-BO algorithm that utilizes related-task information when determined useful, falling back to lookahead otherwise, within a unified framework. We demonstrate competitiveness of our method with existing approaches on function optimization tasks, while retaining strong performance in low task-relatedness regimes where test tasks share limited structure with the meta-training set.

</details>


### [145] [Sample Complexity of Autoregressive Reasoning: Chain-of-Thought vs. End-to-End](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12013)
*Steve Hanneke,Idan Mehalel,Shay Moran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了自回归语言模型在两种监督方式下的学习复杂度：端到端监督的样本复杂度可以是常数到线性之间的任意增长率，而思维链监督则能完全消除对生成长度的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自回归语言模型的学习复杂性，特别是样本复杂度如何依赖于生成长度T，以及思维链监督能否减少这种依赖。这是对Joshi等人提出的自回归模型PAC学习框架的深入探索。

Method: 采用PAC学习框架分析自回归模型的学习复杂性。引入新的组合工具，对端到端学习和思维链监督两种场景进行理论分析，建立样本复杂度与生成长度T的关系。

Result: 1. 端到端学习：在温和条件下，样本复杂度可以是常数到线性之间的任意增长率r(T)，结合Joshi等人的线性上界，得到了几乎完整的刻画。2. 思维链监督：样本复杂度与T无关，表明访问中间推理步骤能完全消除对生成长度的依赖。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了自回归模型学习复杂性的分类学：端到端学习具有丰富的复杂度景观，而思维链监督能显著简化学习过程，完全消除对生成长度的依赖。这解决了Joshi等人提出的关于生成长度依赖性和思维链监督作用的几个开放性问题。

Abstract: Modern large language models generate text autoregressively, producing tokens one at a time. To study the learnability of such systems, Joshi et al. (COLT 2025) introduced a PAC-learning framework for next-token generators, the primitive underlying autoregressive models. In this framework, an unknown next-token generator maps a sequence of tokens to the next token and is iteratively applied for $T$ steps, producing a chain of tokens whose final token constitutes the model's output. The learning task is to learn the input-output mapping induced by this autoregressive process. Depending on the available supervision, training examples may reveal only the final output (End-to-End supervision) or the entire generated chain (Chain-of-Thought supervision). This raises two natural questions: how the sample complexity depends on the generation length $T$, and how much Chain-of-Thought supervision can reduce this dependence.
  In this work we give a nearly complete answer to both questions by uncovering a taxonomy of how the sample complexity scales with $T$. For End-to-End learning, we show that the landscape is remarkably rich: subject to mild conditions, essentially any growth rate $r(T)$ between constant and linear can arise as the sample complexity, and combined with the linear upper bound of Joshi et al., this yields an essentially complete characterization. In contrast, under Chain-of-Thought supervision we show that the sample complexity is independent of $T$, demonstrating that access to intermediate reasoning steps can eliminate the dependence on the generation length altogether. Our analysis introduces new combinatorial tools, and as corollaries we resolve several open questions posed by Joshi et al. regarding the dependence of learnability on the generation length and the role of Chain-of-Thought supervision.

</details>


### [146] [UCS: Estimating Unseen Coverage for Improved In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12015)
*Jiayi Xin,Xiang Li,Evan Qiang,Weiqing He,Tianqi Shang,Weijie J. Su,Qi Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出UCS方法，基于未覆盖聚类选择原则，通过模型一致性嵌入和Good-Turing估计器优化演示选择，提升上下文学习性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演示选择方法主要依赖启发式的相关性或多样性概念，对演示集的覆盖范围提供有限洞察，需要更系统的覆盖度评估方法

Method: 提出UCS方法：1) 从模型一致性嵌入中诱导离散潜在聚类；2) 使用平滑Good-Turing估计器通过经验频率谱估计候选子集中未揭示的聚类数量；3) 通过正则化目标与现有选择基线无缝结合

Result: 在多个意图分类和推理基准测试中，UCS增强的基线方法在相同选择预算下将ICL准确率提升2-6%，同时揭示了任务和模型级别的潜在聚类分布

Conclusion: UCS作为一种基于覆盖度、无需训练的选择方法，通过暴露模型未见的潜在聚类，有效提升了上下文学习性能，为演示选择提供了新的视角

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) performance depends critically on which demonstrations are placed in the prompt, yet most existing selectors prioritize heuristic notions of relevance or diversity and provide limited insight into the coverage of a demonstration set. We propose Unseen Coverage Selection (UKS), a training-free, subset-level coverage prior motivated by the principle that a good demonstration set should expose the model to latent cluster unrevealed by the currently selected subset. UCS operationalizes this idea by (1) inducing discrete latent clusters from model-consistent embeddings and (2) estimating the number of unrevealed clusters within a candidate subset via a Smoothed Good--Turing estimator from its empirical frequency spectrum. Unlike previous selection methods, UCS is coverage-based and training-free, and can be seamlessly combined with both query-dependent and query-independent selection baselines via a simple regularized objective. Experiments on multiple intent-classification and reasoning benchmarks with frontier Large Language Models show that augmenting strong baselines with UCS consistently improves ICL accuracy by up to 2-6% under the same selection budget, while also yielding insights into task- and model-level latent cluster distributions. Code is available at https://github.com/Raina-Xin/UCS.

</details>


### [147] [VISTA: Validation-Informed Trajectory Adaptation via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.12044)
*Eli Corn,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VISTA是一个在线自蒸馏框架，通过识别早期模型状态作为专家锚点，在训练过程中集成这些锚点的覆盖加权集成，防止模型放弃已掌握的知识，从而解决轨迹偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型可能在验证精度较高的情况下仍收敛到次优解，这种现象被称为轨迹偏差。模型在训练过程中会放弃对特定数据子群体已掌握的高泛化状态，丢弃先前学习的潜在特征，而不会触发经典过拟合信号。

Method: VISTA框架：1) 使用验证信息边际覆盖分数识别专家锚点（早期模型状态，保留对特定数据区域的专门能力）；2) 在训练过程中在线集成这些锚点的覆盖加权集成；3) 通过正则化损失景观来保留已掌握的知识。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，VISTA相比标准训练和先前的自蒸馏方法表现出更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力。轻量级实现将存储开销减少了90%且无性能损失。

Conclusion: VISTA通过在线自蒸馏和轨迹一致性约束，有效解决了深度学习中的轨迹偏差问题，提高了模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力，同时通过轻量级实现显著降低了存储成本。

Abstract: Deep learning models may converge to suboptimal solutions despite strong validation accuracy, masking an optimization failure we term Trajectory Deviation.
